### `dismiss-attention`

Close one Inbox item for the person. Only a client attached right now can do it, with the nonce from its attach reply: an agent cannot clear what is waiting for the person. Dismissing a finished item marks the pane's turns seen, and dismissing mail marks the person's mail in the thread read. An item of a linked host is hidden on this daemon only, until that host changes it; nothing on that host is marked.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string |  |  | The item id list-attention printed. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The nonce the daemon issued in an attach reply, for a client attached now over the same kind of connection. The TUI sends its own. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | The item that was closed. |
| `kind` | string | Its kind. One of `approval`, `plan`, `ask`, `question`, `mail`, `errored`, `resume`, `finished`, `outbox` or `held_mail`. |
| `session` | string | Its session. |
| `host` | string | The linked host the item came from. Omitted for this machine. |
| `dismissed` | bool | Always true on success. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"dismiss-attention","params":{"id":"17","human_nonce":"<from the attach reply>"}}
```

### `display-env`

Report the display a new pane of the session gets now: WAYLAND_DISPLAY naming the session's Wayland link, and DISPLAY and XAUTHORITY of the newest local client. WAYLAND_DISPLAY is there also before a client attaches, and left out when the session's clients have an X11 display and no Wayland socket. It is empty unless [daemon] display is follow and the daemon has no display of its own.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session whose display to report. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | Session name. |
| `follow` | bool | True when [daemon] display is follow and the daemon has no display of its own. |
| `own` | bool | True when the daemon has a WAYLAND_DISPLAY or DISPLAY of its own. Its panes keep it, and the follow is off. |
| `link` | string | The session's Wayland link. It exists only while an attached client has a Wayland socket. |
| `target` | string | The client socket the link points at. Absent when there is no link. |
| `env` | object | The variables a new pane gets: WAYLAND_DISPLAY, DISPLAY and XAUTHORITY, each only when set. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"display-env","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

### `drop-files`

Give a pane on a machine the paths of files on this machine. For this machine the answer is the paths. For a host, the files are copied into a new private drop folder there, and the answer has their paths there and the transfers that copy them. Paste a path once its transfer is done.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `host` | string |  |  | The machine the pane is on. Omit for this machine. |
| `paths` | []string | yes |  | The files on this machine. |
| `dir` | string |  |  | A folder on the host to copy into instead of a drop folder, such as the pane's own folder. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `paths` | []string | The files' paths on the pane's machine, in order. |
| `dir` | string | The folder they go into on the host. |
| `transfers` | []object | One transfer row per file, as transfer-list. Empty for this machine. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"drop-files","params":{"host":"build","paths":["~/Desktop/screenshot.png"]}}
```

### `exec`

Run one shell line on this machine and return its stdout, its stderr and how it ended. No window is opened. The line runs under sh -c (cmd /c on Windows) in a process group of its own, with no stdin and the daemon's environment less the pane, session and agent variables of the command that started the daemon, as a pane gets it. The group is stopped at the timeout, when the caller goes away, and when the daemon stops. Each stream keeps its last 1 MiB. At most 8 run at once. Over a link it needs open, as new-window does.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `line` | string | yes |  | The command line, parsed by the shell, at most 16384 bytes. |
| `cwd` | string |  |  | Directory to run in. It must exist. Omit for the daemon's home directory. |
| `timeout_ms` | int |  |  | How long the line may run, in milliseconds, at most one hour. Omit for 30000. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `stdout` | bytes | What the line wrote to stdout, base64 in JSON. |
| `stderr` | bytes | What the line wrote to stderr, base64 in JSON. |
| `stdout_dropped` | int | Bytes cut from the start of stdout to keep the last 1 MiB. |
| `stderr_dropped` | int | Bytes cut from the start of stderr to keep the last 1 MiB. |
| `exit` | int | The exit status. Absent when the line was stopped by a signal, the timeout or the caller leaving. |
| `signal` | string | The signal that stopped the line, such as killed. Absent when it exited. |
| `signal_number` | int | The number of that signal, such as 9. |
| `timed_out` | bool | The timeout stopped the line. |
| `duration_ms` | int | How long the line ran. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"exec","params":{"line":"uname -a"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"exec","params":{"line":"ls -la","cwd":"/tmp","timeout_ms":60000}}
```

### `explain-agent-detect`

Say in plain words whether a pane runs an agent and on what evidence. Lists every agent name seen on the command line that did not count, what the detector read (comm, argv, executable, and the processes behind a wrapper), which harness manifest matched and on which predicate, and for each manifest that did not match, what it compared against.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | The window explained. |
| `verdict` | string | One sentence: whether the pane runs an agent, and which. |
| `evidence` | []string | The facts the verdict rests on, one sentence each. |
| `state` | string | The agent state the pane shows. One of `none`, `working`, `needs_input`, `idle`, `done`, `errored`, `unknown` or `limit_reached`. |
| `source` | string | The source that owns the state, empty for a pane nothing has claimed. |
| `harness_id` | string | The harness the pane shows, empty when none is named. |
| `auto_detected` | bool | Whether the foreground detector promoted the pane. |
| `identity` | string | What named the agent: report (the harness named itself), manifest (a manifest rule matched the process), list (a name list matched the process) or hint (TUIOS_AGENT in the process environment). Empty when nothing named it. One of `report`, `manifest`, `list`, `hint` or `(empty)`. |
| `confidence` | string | How sure the identity is: certain for report, strong for manifest, list and hint, none when nothing named the agent. One of `certain`, `strong` or `none`. |
| `evidence_age_ms` | int or null | Milliseconds since the last evidence about the state arrived. For a state the agent or a rule reported, that is the report. For a state the detector or the silence timer inferred (source detect or stall), it is the later of that and the pane's last output. A look that reads back the same claim does not reset it. null when nothing ever set a state. |
| `running` | bool | Whether a foreground process could be read. |
| `matched` | bool | Whether the process read now matches an agent. |
| `matched_rule` | string | With matched: the predicate that matched. |
| `matched_harness` | string | With matched: the harness the match names, empty for a name-list match. |
| `matched_via` | []string | With matched: the wrappers the detector read through to find the agent, outermost first. Empty when the foreground process itself matched. |
| `name_list` | string | The name-list rule the process matches, when one does. |
| `note` | string | A caveat on the verdict, such as a name-list match that names no harness. |
| `reason` | string | With running false: why no process could be read. |
| `group` | []object | The processes read behind a wrapper: pid, depth, comm, argv, exe and matched for each. |
| `process` | object | What the detector read: comm, argv and executable. |
| `manifests` | []object | Each manifest and what its rules made of the process. |
| `ignored` | []string | Words that look like an agent's name and did not count, each with the reason. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"explain-agent-detect","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

### `explain-agent-screen`

Show a pane's screen tail exactly as the harness screen rules read it, what every rule made of it, and which one fired. Use it to write or debug a rule: for each rule that did not match, it names the strings and nested groups that were the reason, and a rule reading a region narrower than the tail carries the text it read there. The pane's window title, its last OSC 9;4 progress report and what the title rules made of them are reported alongside, since a title is gone from the screen by the time anyone asks why a pane reads the way it does. manifest_source names the manifest file in force, and replaces_bundled says a user file took a bundled manifest's place.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `harness` | string |  |  | Run this harness's rules instead of the pane's own. Use it to try rules against a pane no harness has claimed. |
| `lines` | int |  |  | Read this many lines from the bottom instead of the manifest's count. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"explain-agent-screen","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"explain-agent-screen","params":{"session":"work","harness":"codex","lines":20}}
```

### `fan`

Fan a prompt out across several agents. Creates count worktrees and sessions, starts an agent in each, and types each session's prompt into its agent once the agent shows it is at its prompt. Returns as soon as the sessions exist. An agent that is not ready for 30 seconds turns its prompt_status to held and raises a question in the Inbox; the prompt is still typed when it is ready.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `count` | int |  |  | How many worktrees and agents, 1 to 16. Required unless prompts is given, which sets it. |
| `agent` | string |  |  | The agent to run in every session, as one string: a harness id or program name (claude, codex, gemini), or any program, with its arguments after it ("codex --model o5"). Words are split the way a shell splits them, quotes included, and exec'd directly; nothing is expanded. Required unless agents is given. |
| `agents` | []string |  |  | Several agents, each written as agent is, cycled across the sessions: ["claude","codex"] with count 4 is claude, codex, claude, codex. Takes no agent. |
| `prompt` | string |  |  | The prompt every agent gets. It is typed as one paste (wrapped in bracketed paste when the agent has it on) and submitted with a carriage return, the way ask-agent types a question. Required unless prompts is given. |
| `prompts` | []string |  |  | One prompt per session, in order. count defaults to how many there are and must equal it when given. Takes no prompt. |
| `repo` | string |  |  | A directory inside the repository. Required unless repo_url is passed. |
| `base` | string |  |  | Ref every branch starts from. Omit for HEAD of the main checkout. |
| `name` | string |  |  | Branch stem, which names the fan. The branches are the stem, then stem-2, stem-3 and so on. A name that is already a fan in the repository is refused with fan_exists unless join is given. Omit for fan/ and the first words of the first prompt, which moves on to a free name. A name can have only A-Z, a-z, 0-9 and / . _ -. Any other character is refused. |
| `join` | string |  |  | A session of an existing fan to add the attempts to. The caller must reach that session. Omit to start a new fan. |
| `ready_timeout` | int |  | `600000` | Milliseconds to wait for each agent to be ready before giving up on its prompt. |
| `env` | object |  |  | Environment variables for every agent, name to value, on top of the daemon's. PATH in it is where the programs are looked up, so an agent the caller can run is found. The tuios CLI sends its PATH. TUIOS_ names, TMUX and TMUX_PANE are refused, and a call from another machine may not pass env at all. Not saved: a restored pane starts with the daemon's environment. |
| `task` | string |  |  | A task card the fan works on, such as t3. The card gets the fan's group and fan_id, and moves from todo or plan to doing. It keeps no holder, since the fan's attempts work on it, and claim-task next skips it while the fan has a session. A fan check and the attempts' pull requests then move it (see [tasks] auto_stage). The caller needs what claim-task needs, and the card must be held by no pane but the caller's, not blocked and not done, checked before anything is made. |
| `task_session` | string |  |  | The session of task. Omit for the caller's own session, or outside every pane the most recently active one. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | With task: the attached client's nonce, so the stamp is the person's (by human). |
| `model` | string |  |  | The model for every agent, passed with the flag each harness's manifest names ([launch] model_flag). Refused when one of the agents takes no model flag. |
| `effort` | string |  |  | The effort for every agent, one of each harness's [launch] efforts. |
| `permission_mode` | string |  |  | The permission mode for every agent, one of each harness's [launch] permissions. From an agent pane it is held to the caller's own mode. |
| `grants` | []string |  |  | What the new pane may do through tuios: read, write, fan, respond, admin, or none. Omit for the default of [agents.permissions], or, from a pane without admin, the calling pane's own grants. A pane may give only what it holds, and admin does not include respond. A window on another machine holds what that machine gives it. One of `read`, `write`, `fan`, `respond`, `files`, `task`, `admin` or `none`. |
| `setup` | string |  |  | A shell line to run with sh -c in each new worktree, in a window named setup, before the agent or shell starts. It holds no grants. Omit for [worktree] setup in config.toml. An empty string runs none. |
| `setup_async` | bool |  |  | Start the agent at once beside the setup. Omit for [worktree] setup_async. Without it the agent waits for the setup to pass, and a failed setup removes the new worktree and the branch it made when nothing in them is anyone's work. A failed async setup keeps both. |
| `repo_url` | string |  |  | The repository by its origin URL, for a caller on another machine. This daemon finds its own checkout whose origin is the same repository. Pass repo or repo_url, not both. |
| `repos_root` | string |  |  | With repo_url, the directory on this machine to look under, and to clone into. Absolute, or starting with ~/. Omit to look under ~/src, ~/dev, ~/code, ~/projects, ~/repos, ~/git, ~/work, ~/go/src, the home directory itself and tuios's own clone directory. |
| `clone` | bool |  | `false` | With repo_url, clone the repository when no checkout is found. Only https, ssh and git URLs are cloned. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `group` | string | The branch stem, which is the group's name in list-worktrees. |
| `repo` | string | The repository's name. |
| `repo_root` | string | The repository's main checkout on this machine. |
| `cloned` | bool | True when the repository was cloned by this call. Absent otherwise. |
| `agent` | string | The harness id started in the first session, empty for a program no manifest recognises. |
| `command` | string | The command started in the first session. |
| `prompt` | string | The first session's prompt, as given. |
| `sessions` | []object | One entry per session: session, branch, path, window_id, and the agent (harness id) and command started in it. hook_error is there when that session's after-new-worktree hook failed. setup is there when the session runs a setup, and agent_pending with no window_id while a sync setup holds the agent. |
| `total` | int | How many sessions were started. |
| `task` | object | With task: the card as stamped, as list-tasks gives a row. |
| `task_note` | string | With task: why nothing was stamped, when the card was removed while the fan was made. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"fan","params":{"count":3,"agent":"claude","prompt":"Add a retry to the client.","repo":"/src/api"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"fan","params":{"count":3,"agents":["claude","codex --model o5","gemini"],"prompt":"Add a retry to the client.","repo":"/src/api","env":{"PATH":"/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin"}}}
```

### `file-abort`

Remove the part a copy to a path left on this machine.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The file the copy was for. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `part_id` | string |  |  | The copy's part id, up to 32 hex digits: the part is .NAME.tuios-part-ID, so two copies to one path write two parts. Omit for .NAME.tuios-part. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The path. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-abort","params":{"path":"~/big.iso"}}
```

### `file-check`

Describe many files under a folder on this machine at once, for a copy that leaves out what is there already: whether each is there, its kind, size and time, and with hash its sha256. At most 2000 files, and 512 MiB hashed, in one call.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `root` | string | yes |  | The folder. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `rels` | []string | yes |  | The files, as paths under the folder, slash separated. |
| `hash` | bool |  |  | Hash each regular file. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `entries` | []object | One per file, in order: rel, exists, kind (file, dir, symlink, other), size, mtime (Unix ms), sha256 when hashed. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-check","params":{"root":"~/src","rels":["a.go","b/c.go"],"hash":true}}
```

### `file-commit`

Finish a copy written with open-file-stream: check the part's sha256 against the sender's, then rename it into place. A part that does not match is removed.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The file the copy is for. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `sha256` | string |  |  | The sender's hash, hex. Omit to skip the check. |
| `conflict` | string |  | `fail` | When the path exists: replace it, keep both (the copy gets a new name), skip (the part is removed and the file there stays), or fail. One of `replace`, `keep-both`, `skip` or `fail`. |
| `label` | string |  |  | keep-both names the copy "name (LABEL).ext", such as "report (from build).pdf". Omit to name it "name 2.ext". |
| `perm` | int |  |  | The original's permission bits, which the finished file gets. Omit to keep it owner only. |
| `mtime` | int |  |  | The original's modification time, Unix ms, which the finished file gets. Omit to keep the time of the copy. |
| `part_id` | string |  |  | The copy's part id, up to 32 hex digits: the part is .NAME.tuios-part-ID, so two copies to one path write two parts. Omit for .NAME.tuios-part. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | Where the file is now. |
| `sha256` | string | The part's hash. |
| `skipped` | bool | conflict skip found a file at path, so the copy was removed and the file there stays. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-commit","params":{"path":"~/big.iso","sha256":"9f86d0...","conflict":"keep-both"}}
```

### `file-drop-dir`

Make a private folder on this machine for files dropped on a pane, and answer its path. Drops are kept for 24 hours and 256 MiB together, oldest removed first.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bytes` | int |  |  | The size of the drop, so older drops make room for it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `dir` | string | The new folder, owner only. |
| `keep_bytes` | int | How many bytes the drops together may hold. |
| `keep_hours` | int | How long a drop is kept. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-drop-dir","params":{"bytes":1048576}}
```

### `file-hash`

The sha256 of a file on this machine, of a range of it, or of the part a copy to it is writing.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The file. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `offset` | int |  | `0` | Where the range starts. |
| `length` | int |  |  | How many bytes. Omit for the rest of the file. |
| `part` | bool |  |  | Hash the part file of a copy to this path. |
| `part_id` | string |  |  | The copy's part id, up to 32 hex digits: the part is .NAME.tuios-part-ID, so two copies to one path write two parts. Omit for .NAME.tuios-part. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `sha256` | string | The hash, hex. |
| `bytes` | int | How many bytes were hashed. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-hash","params":{"path":"~/big.iso"}}
```

### `file-list`

List a folder on this machine a page at a time, folders first, then names in natural order (file2 before file10), with size, time and mode.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dir` | string |  |  | The folder. Omit for the home folder. |
| `offset` | int |  | `0` | The first entry of the page. |
| `limit` | int |  | `500` | Entries in the page, at most 5000. |
| `hidden` | bool |  | `true` | Include names that start with a dot. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `dir` | string | The folder, made absolute. |
| `entries` | []object | One info object per entry, as file-stat's info. |
| `total` | int | Entries in the folder. |
| `next` | int | The offset of the next page. Absent on the last page. |
| `parent` | string | The folder above. Absent at the root. |
| `home` | string | This machine's home folder. |
| `capped` | bool | The folder holds more than 50000 names, and only those were read. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-list","params":{"dir":"~/src","limit":200}}
```

### `file-mkdir`

Make a folder on this machine, with its parents.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The folder. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `receive` | bool |  |  | Make the receive folder instead, and ignore path: where a copy from the caller lands when it names no folder. For a linked machine it is the first folder of its files_roots, else ~/Downloads/tuios. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The folder, made absolute. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-mkdir","params":{"path":"~/new"}}
```

```json
{"id":2,"verb":"file-mkdir","params":{"path":"~","receive":true}}
```

### `file-preview`

Make the preview of a file on this machine. An image comes scaled to max_px on its long side, a PDF page and a film frame as an image (with pdftoppm and ffmpeg there), text as its first 256 KiB, and anything else as its type and size.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The file. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `max_px` | int |  | `1600` | The longest side of an image, at most 4096. |
| `page` | int |  | `1` | The PDF page. |
| `pages_known` | bool |  | `false` | The caller knows the page count, so pdfinfo does not run. |
| `words` | bool |  | `false` | Send the PDF page's words from pdftotext in place of its picture. |
| `at_ms` | int |  |  | The position in a film of the frame to send. Without it the frame is 5 % in. |
| `duration_known` | bool |  | `false` | The caller knows the film's facts, so ffprobe does not run. |
| `no_frame` | bool |  | `false` | Send only the film's facts. |
| `format` | bool |  | `false` | Lay out a JSON file for reading. |
| `details` | bool |  | `false` | Send the file's permissions, what git says of it, its diff and a PDF page's words, in place of the preview. |
| `offset` | int |  |  | Send a window of a text file from this byte, whole lines only, in place of the preview. |
| `limit` | int |  | `262144` | The most bytes in that window, at most 1 MiB. |
| `find` | string |  |  | Look for this text in a text file, in place of the preview. Lower case ignores case. |
| `from` | int |  |  | The byte find starts at. |
| `back` | bool |  |  | Find goes toward the start of the file. |
| `line` | int |  |  | Find the start of this line number, in place of the preview. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `kind` | string | image, svg, pdf, video, text, dir or binary. One of `image`, `svg`, `pdf`, `video`, `text`, `dir` or `binary`. |
| `image` | string | The picture, base64, as mime says: the image, the page or the frame. |
| `mime` | string | The picture's type for image, pdf and video, and the file's type otherwise. |
| `width` | int | An image's own width. |
| `height` | int | An image's own height. |
| `text` | string | text and svg: the content. |
| `truncated` | bool | text: the file is longer than what was sent. |
| `offset` | int | A window: the byte it starts at. find and line: the start of the line found, or -1. |
| `reached` | int | find and line: how far the read went. Call again from there when the search ran out of time. |
| `counted` | int | line: the lines passed. |
| `next_offset` | int | A window: the byte the next window starts at. |
| `pdf` | string | pdf, when this machine cannot draw the page: the file, base64, at most 8 MiB. |
| `pages` | int | pdf: the page count, when pdfinfo is there. |
| `words` | string | pdf, when asked for: the page's words. |
| `no_tool` | string | pdf: the tool this machine needs and does not have. |
| `at_ms` | int | video: the position of the frame sent. |
| `formatted` | bool | text: the JSON was laid out. |
| `entries` | array | archive: the listing, with name, size and dir. |
| `capped` | bool | archive: the listing stopped at a bound. |
| `head` | string | binary: the first 16 KiB, base64. |
| `details` | bool | The reply holds the details asked for. |
| `mode` | string | details: the permissions. |
| `owner` | string | details: the file's owner. |
| `group` | string | details: the file's group. |
| `git` | string | details: what git says of the file. |
| `diff` | string | details: the diff of a changed file. |
| `video_codec` | string | video: the codec, from ffprobe. |
| `audio_codec` | string | video: the codec of the sound, from ffprobe, when it has sound. |
| `fps` | number | video: the frames each second, from ffprobe. |
| `duration_ms` | int | video: the length. |
| `note` | string | Why there is no picture, in words a person can act on. |
| `size` | int | The file's size. |
| `mtime` | int | The file's modification time, Unix ms. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-preview","params":{"path":"~/Pictures/cat.jpg","max_px":1200}}
```

### `file-read`

Read up to 4 MiB of a file on this machine, base64.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The file. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `offset` | int |  | `0` | Where to start. |
| `length` | int |  | `4194304` | How many bytes, at most 4 MiB. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `content` | string | The bytes, base64. |
| `size` | int | The file's size. |
| `eof` | bool | The read reached the end of the file. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-read","params":{"path":"~/notes.md","length":65536}}
```

Delete a path on this machine. A folder that holds files needs recursive. The home folder and / are refused.

| `path` | string | The path deleted. |

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `from` | string | yes |  | The path to move. |
| `to` | string | yes |  | The new path. |
| `replace` | bool |  |  | Replace what is at to. |

| `to` | string | The new path. |

### `file-stat`

Describe one path on this machine. With part, describe the part file a copy to the path is writing.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The path. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `part` | bool |  |  | Describe the part file of a copy to this path instead. |
| `part_id` | string |  |  | The copy's part id, up to 32 hex digits: the part is .NAME.tuios-part-ID, so two copies to one path write two parts. Omit for .NAME.tuios-part. |
| `relation_to` | string |  |  | Another path on this machine. The reply says how path stands to it, with links followed. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The path, made absolute. |
| `exists` | bool | False when nothing is there. |
| `size` | int | The size in bytes. 0 for a part that is not there. |
| `info` | object | name, path, kind (file, dir, symlink, other), link_kind and link for a link, size, mtime (Unix ms), mode (drwxr-xr-x), perm (the permission bits as a number), hidden. |
| `relation` | string | With relation_to: same when the two paths are one file, inside when path is in the folder relation_to, apart otherwise. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-stat","params":{"path":"~/notes.md"}}
```

### `file-walk`

List every file and folder under a folder on this machine, for a folder copy. At most 20000 entries in one reply; with stream, up to 1000000 entries, one JSON line each after the reply, and a last line {end, bytes, skipped, skipped_count, denied} or {end, code, error}. Links are not followed: a link, a named pipe, a socket and a device are not copied, and skipped names them. Over a link, an entry in the read deny list is left out and not named, and denied counts it.

The connection stays open after the reply when `stream` is `true`.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes |  | The folder. An absolute path, or one that starts with ~. |
| `stream` | bool |  |  | Send the entries after the reply, one JSON line each, for a folder of more than 20000 entries. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `entries` | []object | rel (the path under the folder, slash separated), size, dir, perm, mtime (Unix ms). A folder comes before what it holds. |
| `bytes` | int | The bytes of every file together. |
| `skipped` | []object | The first 200 items a copy does not carry: rel and kind (symlink, pipe, socket, device). |
| `skipped_count` | int | How many items a copy does not carry, all of them. |
| `denied` | int | Over a link, how many entries were left out because a linked machine may not read them. A folder counts once. They are not named. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"file-walk","params":{"path":"~/photos"}}
```

### `finish-task`

Mark a task card done. Its holder lets it go and its block, if any, is lifted. Finishing a card that is done already changes nothing. A pane changes only the card it holds unless it holds admin; the person, a shell, a link and a plugin change any card.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `id` | string | yes |  | The card, such as t1. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The attached client's nonce, to act as the person (by human). One that does not verify is refused with not_human. |
| `host` | string |  |  | The machine that owns the card, for a project card claimed, released or finished from the pane that works on it here. The call is passed on over the link for this pane. |
| `remote_window` | string |  |  | Over the link only: the window on the calling machine that holds the card. Its daemon sends it; the holder's machine is the link's peer. |
| `remote_name` | string |  |  | Over the link only: how that window reads on its machine. |
| `remote_harness` | string |  |  | Over the link only: the agent that window runs. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | The card. |
| `session` | string | Its session. |
| `title` | string | Its title. |
| `preview` | string | The first line of its prompt, at most 80 characters. |
| `stage` | string | Its stage. One of `todo`, `plan`, `doing`, `verify`, `review`, `shipped` or `done`. |
| `stage_at` | int | When it entered the stage, Unix nanoseconds. |
| `holder` | object | The pane that holds it: window, name, harness, state (read from the pane now) and held_at. Null when no pane holds it. |
| `by` | string | Who added it: human, shell, the adding pane's window id, link:HOST or plugin:ID. |
| `at` | int | When it was added, Unix nanoseconds. |
| `blocked` | object | reason, by and at when the card is blocked, else null. |
| `fan` | object | fan and fan_id, for a card added in a fan's session. |
| `attachment_count` | int | How many attachments the card has. Absent for none. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"finish-task","params":{"session":"work","id":"t1"}}
```

### `get-agent-state`

Read the agent state a window's pane last reported, with its optional message, the time it was set, which source and harness it came from, how confident the harness attribution is, whether the pane needs a person, whether ask-agent would type at it now (ready), and for a pane on needs_input whether it waits on an approval or a question (blocked_by).

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | The window the state is read from. |
| `state` | string | The agent state the pane shows. One of `none`, `working`, `needs_input`, `idle`, `done`, `errored`, `unknown` or `limit_reached`. |
| `reason` | string | Why state is not the state the agent gave: silent when its hooks reported working and then sent no report for agents.silent_after, which shows the pane as unknown until the next report. Empty otherwise. |
| `message` | string | The message the state was reported with, empty when none. |
| `agent_state_at` | int | When the state was last set, in Unix nanoseconds. 0 when nothing ever set one. |
| `source` | string | The source that owns the state: report, transcript, osc, screen, detect or stall. |
| `harness_id` | string | The harness the source named, empty when none did. |
| `identity` | string | What named the agent: report (the harness named itself), manifest (a manifest rule matched the process), list (a name list matched the process) or hint (TUIOS_AGENT in the process environment). Empty when nothing named it. One of `report`, `manifest`, `list`, `hint` or `(empty)`. |
| `confidence` | string | How sure the identity is: certain for report, strong for manifest, list and hint, none when nothing named the agent. One of `certain`, `strong` or `none`. |
| `evidence_age_ms` | int or null | Milliseconds since the last evidence about the state arrived. For a state the agent or a rule reported, that is the report. For a state the detector or the silence timer inferred (source detect or stall), it is the later of that and the pane's last output. A look that reads back the same claim does not reset it. null when nothing ever set a state. |
| `needs_you` | bool | Whether the state asks for a person: true for needs_input and errored. |
| `activity` | string | The state in one word: working, waiting, resting, unknown or none. |
| `ready` | bool | Whether ask-agent would type at the pane now. |
| `blocked_by` | string | approval or question for a pane on needs_input, empty otherwise. |
| `waits_on` | string | What a done or idle agent still runs after its turn, read from its screen, such as 1 background shell. Empty otherwise. |
| `agent_session_id` | string | The harness's own conversation id, empty until a hook reports one. |
| `meta` | object | The set-agent-meta keys, key to value. |
| `queued` | int | How many messages wait in the pane's delivery queue. |
| `settled` | bool | Whether the person put the agent away with mark-attention settle. The daemon clears it when the agent needs the person again. |
| `subagents` | int | How many subagents the pane's agent is running, as its hooks reported them with report-agent-activity. meta's subagents key says it in words. |
| `subagent_list` | []object | The pane's subagents in start order, at most 16, each kept for a minute after it ends (five for a failed one): id, type, description (what it was asked to do), state (running, done, failed or stopped), started_at and ended_at (Unix nanoseconds, ended_at 0 while it runs), now (the tool it runs, such as "Bash: go test ./..."), last (the tool it ran last), tools (how many tool calls it made) and result (the first line it ended with, or the error). The strings are the agent's, cut to one line with likely secrets masked. Empty while there are none. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"get-agent-state","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

| `risk` | []object | The risk rules matched, each with rule and why. |
| `deny_message` | bool | Whether a deny may carry a reason. |
| `untrusted` | bool | Always true: the text is the agent's. |

A stage the daemon moved on its own ([tasks] auto_stage) has by tuios and a reason naming the fact: plan held for approval, agent working, fan check started, fan check failed in every attempt, or the pull request opened, merged or closed. |

### `judge-fan`

Start an agent that judges the attempts of a fan. It runs in a new session of its own, named after the fan with -judge, in the repository's main checkout, with the read grant alone. Once its pane exists, the attempts name that pane, and that pane alone reads them: it cannot type into them, run commands in their sessions or start agents there. Its first prompt, typed then, asks it to read the attempts and report its pick with set-fan-verdict. Only the person or a pane with admin starts a judge. The record is cleared when the judge's session ends. It answers once the agent is ready, as start-agent does.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | yes |  | Any session of the fan. |
| `agent` | string |  |  | The judge's agent, as start-agent takes it. Omit for the agent the first attempt ran. |
| `env` | object |  |  | Environment variables for the judge, as start-agent takes them. |
| `ready_timeout` | int |  | `120000` | Milliseconds to wait for the judge to be ready. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `judge_session` | string | The judge's session. |
| `group` | string | The fan's group. |
| `window_id` | string | The judge's pane, with the rest of what start-agent returns. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"judge-fan","params":{"session":"api-fan-retry"}}
```

### `keep-fan`

Keep one attempt of a fan and remove the others: each sibling's worktree and session go, the way remove-worktree removes them, each on its own. A sibling with uncommitted changes is left in place unless stash or force says what to do with them. Files git ignores are deleted with a worktree and never stashed: compare-fan with detail lists them. Branches are never deleted. With merge, the kept attempt's branch is first merged into its base, as ship-merge does.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | yes |  | The attempt to keep. |
| `stash` | bool |  | `false` | Stash a sibling's uncommitted changes before removing it. |
| `force` | bool |  | `false` | Discard a sibling's uncommitted changes. |
| `merge` | bool |  | `false` | First merge the kept attempt's branch into its base in the main checkout, as ship-merge does. A merge that conflicts or is refused stops the call before any sibling is removed. |
| `merge_mode` | string |  | `merge` | With merge: merge, squash or ff-only, as ship-merge takes mode. One of `merge`, `squash` or `ff-only`. |
| `into` | string |  |  | With merge: the branch to merge into. Omit for the base the attempt was made from. |
| `group` | string |  |  | The fan's group, as compare-fan gave it. When given, a session of another fan is refused. |
| `repo_root` | string |  |  | The fan's repository, as compare-fan gave it. When given, a session of another repository is refused. |
| `fan_id` | string |  |  | The fan's ID, as compare-fan gave it. When given, a session of another fan of the same name is refused. |
| `siblings` | []string |  |  | The attempts the caller showed it would remove. When given, a fan whose other attempts differ is refused whole. |
| `ignored` | object |  |  | Per attempt to remove, the ignored_hash compare-fan gave for the list the caller showed. When given, an attempt whose ignored files changed, or that has no entry, is refused, and nothing is removed. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `kept` | string | The session kept. |
| `branch` | string | The kept session's branch. |
| `group` | string | The fan's group. |
| `removed` | []object | One per sibling: session and removed, with remove-worktree's result when it was removed, or note and code when it was not. |
| `left` | int | How many siblings were not removed. |
| `merge` | object | With merge: what ship-merge returns for the kept attempt. Omitted without merge. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"keep-fan","params":{"session":"api-fan-retry-1"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"keep-fan","params":{"session":"api-fan-retry-1","stash":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"keep-fan","params":{"session":"api-fan-retry-1","merge":true,"merge_mode":"squash"}}
```

### `kill-session`

Terminate a session and every window in it.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session to terminate. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"kill-session","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

### `layout-template`

Save the workspace a session shows as a layout template, load one back, list them, or delete one. Templates are files of this machine (~/.config/tuios/layouts), so every client reads one list. A load reuses the workspace's panes in order, opens the ones it lacks with their folder and name and types their command, minimizes the ones it does not name, and gives a bsp layout its split tree back. Works with no client attached.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | string | yes |  | What to do. One of `list`, `save`, `load` or `delete`. |
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `name` | string |  |  | The layout. Needed to save, load and delete. |
| `workspace` | int |  |  | save: the workspace to save. The one the session shows when absent. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `layouts` | []object | list: each layout's name, panes, mode, tiling, created_at and version. |
| `dir` | string | list: the folder the layouts are kept in. |
| `name` | string | The layout saved, loaded or deleted. |
| `panes` | int | save and load: how many panes the layout holds, or shows. |
| `mode` | string | save and load: the layout mode, bsp, master-stack, scrolling or floating. |
| `workspace` | int | save and load: the workspace. |
| `path` | string | save: the file written. |
| `focus` | string | load: the pane that has the focus after the load, a pane of the layout. |
| `opened` | int | load: panes opened for the layout. |
| `reused` | int | load: panes of the workspace that took a place. |
| `minimized` | int | load: panes of the workspace the layout does not name, minimized. |
| `deleted` | bool | delete: the layout was removed. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"layout-template","params":{"action":"save","session":"work","name":"three-up"}}
```

```json
{"id":2,"verb":"layout-template","params":{"action":"load","session":"work","name":"three-up"}}
```

```json
{"id":3,"verb":"layout-template","params":{"action":"list"}}
```

### `link-peer`

Name the machine a link connection came from. tuios stdio-proxy sends it as the first line of every connection it opens for a link, and the daemon resolves that machine's link policy from the name. Accepted once per connection, before anything else, and only on a link socket. After the reply the connection is read from scratch, JSON or binary.

The connection stays open after the reply.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `peer` | string |  |  | The machine's name: the one the hub gave for itself, or the one the proxy was pinned to with --as. Empty for none. |
| `pinned` | bool |  |  | The name came from stdio-proxy --as on this machine, not from the hub. |
| `ssh_auth_sock` | string |  |  | The SSH_AUTH_SOCK stdio-proxy runs with: the agent the link's ssh forwards. A client attached through the link uses it for ssh_agent = "follow". |
| `closes` | string |  |  | The close_token of another link connection. The far side closed that connection's stream on purpose, so a size lease it holds ends at once and does not wait for a viewer to come back. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `peer` | string | The name the connection is held to. |
| `close_token` | string | The token stdio-proxy sends back as closes when the far side closes this connection's stream on purpose. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"link-peer","params":{"peer":"laptop"}}
```

### `list-agents`

List the agent panes in a session with the state each reports, the harness behind it, where it is working, and how much unread mail is waiting for it. This is how an agent discovers who else is here and what to address.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `all` | bool |  | `false` | Include every window, not only the panes something has identified as an agent. |
| `all_sessions` | bool |  | `false` | List the agent panes of every session on the daemon, in session name order. Takes no session. human_unread is then the person's unread mail over every session. |
| `select` | string |  |  | A selector: space-separated key:value terms, all of which must match, each with comma-separated alternatives. Keys: harness (id or program name), state, needs:you, session (glob), group (fan-out group, glob), host (local or a host name, glob), name (window name, glob), cwd (the directory or under it; ~ is home), task (a task card the pane holds, by id, glob), stage (the stage of a card the pane holds), parent and root (the parent agent pane, or the first of its chain, by window id or a start of it). Keeps only the panes it matches, in every session unless session is also given. The answer then carries the confirm token a write by the same selector takes. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `agents` | []object | One entry per pane: session, window_id, name, state, reason, message, agent_state_at, source, harness_id, foreground, cwd, workspace, focused, unread, seen, ready, blocked_by, needs_you, confidence, identity, evidence_age_ms, completion_seq, finished_unread, agent_session_id, meta, context, limit_resets, group, protocol, queued, settled, subagents, subagent_list. context is how full the agent's context is, null when no source said: tokens (from the transcript, the status line feed or a protocol agent), window (only from a source that states it), percent (only with a window, or when the harness stated it) and near (percent at 80 or more). limit_resets is, for a pane on limit_reached, the reset its harness named, in the harness's words, empty when it named none and for every other state. queued is how many messages wait in the pane's delivery queue. settled is true while the person has put the agent away with mark-attention settle. subagents is how many subagents the pane's agent is running, as its hooks reported them. subagent_list is the subagents themselves, as get-agent-state reports them. ready is whether ask-agent would type at the pane now: true for idle, done, errored, limit_reached and none, false for working, needs_input and unknown. reason is silent for a pane shown as unknown because its hooks reported working and then sent no report for agents.silent_after, empty otherwise. blocked_by is approval or question for a pane on needs_input, empty when the source did not say and for every other state. completion_seq counts the turns the pane finished; finished_unread is true while it is at rest after a turn no attached client has focused it since. agent_session_id is the harness's own conversation id, as a hook reported it. meta is the set-agent-meta keys, key to value. group is the fan-out group of the pane's session, empty outside one. protocol is acp or codex for an agent start-agent runs headless over that protocol, empty for every other pane. parent, root and depth are on a pane another agent started: parent is the agent that started it (session, window, harness, agent_session_id), root is the first agent of the chain (session, window), and depth counts the links from the root. Either carries gone: true once its pane has closed. identity, confidence and evidence_age_ms are as get-agent-state reports them. |
| `total` | int | How many panes are listed. |
| `select` | string | The selector as parsed, when one was given. |
| `confirm` | string | With select, and without all or session: the token for exactly the listed panes, which send-agent-message and ask-agent take as confirm to write to them. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-agents","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-agents","params":{"session":"work","all":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-agents","params":{"all_sessions":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-agents","params":{"select":"harness:codex state:idle,done"}}
```

### `list-attention`

List the Inbox: everything in every session waiting for the person, on this machine and on every linked host. An item is an approval, a plan to approve or a question (a pane on needs_input), a question put with ask-human (kind ask), mail to human, a pane on errored, or a finished turn nobody has looked at. Items are grouped in that order, oldest first inside each group. Every change to the list is an attention event on subscribe.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Only list items in this session. Omit for every session. Unlike most verbs, an omitted session does not mean the most recently active one. Without host it names a session on this machine. |
| `kinds` | []string |  |  | Only list these kinds. Omit for all of them. One of `approval`, `plan`, `ask`, `question`, `mail`, `errored`, `resume`, `finished`, `outbox` or `held_mail`. |
| `host` | string |  |  | Only list items of this machine: "local" for this one, or a linked host by name. Omit for every machine. |
| `select` | string |  |  | A selector: space-separated key:value terms, all of which must match, each with comma-separated alternatives. Keys: harness (id or program name), state, needs:you, session (glob), group (fan-out group, glob), host (local or a host name, glob), name (window name, glob), cwd (the directory or under it; ~ is home), task (a task card the pane holds, by id, glob), stage (the stage of a card the pane holds), parent and root (the parent agent pane, or the first of its chain, by window id or a start of it). Keeps the items it matches. An item's state is the one its kind stands for: needs_input for an approval or a question, errored, and done for finished; mail and resume have none. group and cwd are known only for items of this machine. |
| `include_snoozed` | bool |  | `false` | Also list the items the person snoozed, each with snoozed_until. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `items` | []object | One entry per item: id, kind, host, session, window, workspace, harness, name, summary, options, request_id, expires, always_scope, since, seq, thread, count, completion_seq, stale, seen_at. request_id, expires and always_scope are set only on an approval a hook holds on this machine (see request-approval); options is then the decisions reply-approval takes. An ask item carries request_id and options too: the question's id and its answers, which answer-ask takes. since is when the item started waiting, in unix nanoseconds. seq is the Inbox revision of its last change. summary is one line, with control characters removed, likely secrets masked and at most 160 bytes. thread is the mail thread, and count is the unread messages it stands for (mail) or the turns (finished). seen, on a mail item, is how many of count were seen with a peek and not read. host is empty for this machine; an item of a linked host has an id of the form host:id. stale is true for an item of a host whose link is down, and seen_at is when that host was last heard from, in unix nanoseconds. Additive fields, each absent when unset: snoozed_until on a snoozed item listed with include_snoozed (unix nanoseconds, -1 for until it changes), marked_unread on a finished item the person reopened, risk (the risk rules an approval matched), deny_message (the harness takes a reason with a deny), and plan_lines and plan_sha on a plan item. |
| `counts` | object | Open items per kind over the whole Inbox, every machine included, before the session, kinds and host filters. Every kind is present. |
| `total` | int | How many items are listed. |
| `seq` | int | The event seq the answer is current to. Subscribe with after_seq set to it and the boot_id below, and every attention event after the listing is replayed. |
| `boot_id` | string | The daemon start the seq belongs to. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-attention"}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-attention","params":{"kinds":["approval","question"]}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-attention","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-attention","params":{"host":"build"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-attention","params":{"select":"harness:codex needs:you"}}
```

List the paste buffers, newest first. A yank in copy mode adds one, and so does set-buffer. From a pane this needs the read grant, and a pane without admin sees only the buffers it set. Every total counts only the buffers listed.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sample_width` | int |  | `60` | How many characters each sample shows, at most 200. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `buffers` | []object | One entry per buffer, newest first: name, bytes, created, automatic, sample, session, pane. |
| `total` | int | How many buffers there are. |
| `bytes` | int | How many bytes they hold together. |
| `limit` | int | How many buffers the daemon keeps, from [paste_buffers] limit. |
| `max_bytes` | int | How many bytes the buffers may hold together, from [paste_buffers] max_kb. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-buffers"}
```

### `list-checkpoints`

List the checkpoints of a pane: the state of its git work tree, saved each time its agent finished a turn that changed a file, and before each restore. Oldest first. The labels are the agent's prompts, so the result is marked untrusted.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The root of the work tree the pane is in, symbolic links resolved. |
| `enabled` | bool | Whether checkpoints are taken: [agents.checkpoints] enabled. |
| `keep` | int | How many checkpoints one pane keeps: [agents.checkpoints] keep. |
| `checkpoints` | []object | One entry per checkpoint: n, ref, commit, tree, head, kind (turn or safety), pane, session, turn, state, label, worktree, at (Unix nanoseconds), and skipped and skipped_count (the untracked files left out as larger than max_untracked_mb, omitted when none). |
| `untrusted` | bool | Always true: the labels are the agent's text. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-checkpoints","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

### `list-glyphs`

List the glyph sets and describe one: the roles it names, and the characters that would actually be drawn if it were selected. A glyph set is the shape half of a rice, the way a theme is the colour half, and like a theme its value is a name from an open set standing for a document kept elsewhere.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `glyphs` | string |  |  | Describe this set as well as listing. Omit to list only. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `sets` | []string | Every set id, built-ins first and then the user's. |
| `roles` | []string | Every role a set can name, which is what to write in a set file. |
| `total` | int | How many sets there are. |
| `glyphs_dir` | string | Directory user sets are read from; write <id>.json there. |
| `active` | string | The set in effect, with active_source saying whether it came from the session, config.toml or the default. |
| `problems` | []string | One line per set file that could not be read and per role dropped for being the wrong width. Present only when there are any. |
| `set` | object | Present when glyphs was given: id, display_name, inherits, ascii, names (the roles the set states) and drawn (the character each role would actually render as, defaults folded in). |


### `list-host-agents`

List the agent panes of every session on this machine and on every configured host. Hosts are asked at once, so a slow one costs only its own entry.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `host` | string |  |  | One host by name, or "local" for this machine. Omit for every host. |
| `all` | bool |  |  | List every window on each host, not just the panes identified as agents. |
| `select` | string |  |  | A selector: space-separated key:value terms, all of which must match, each with comma-separated alternatives. Keys: harness (id or program name), state, needs:you, session (glob), group (fan-out group, glob), host (local or a host name, glob), name (window name, glob), cwd (the directory or under it; ~ is home), task (a task card the pane holds, by id, glob), stage (the stage of a card the pane holds), parent and root (the parent agent pane, or the first of its chain, by window id or a start of it). Keeps only the rows it matches, on every host; a host term matches the entry's host. A host from before selectors sends no group, so a group term matches none of its rows. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `hosts` | []string | One entry per host, local first, carrying the agent rows of every session on it, each row naming its session. session is set only when every row is in one session. An entry that failed carries an error and a code; when the host answered before, it also carries the rows it last gave, with stale true and fetched_at in unix seconds. events says how this daemon follows the host: live, polling, or empty while the link is down. |
| `select` | string | The selector as parsed, when one was given. |

No params.

| `modifiers` | []object | Every modifier, as {name, spellings}. super reaches only an attached client's window manager, never a pane. |
| `ctrl_characters` | []string | The characters other than letters that ctrl combines with. |
| `escape_prefixes` | []string | The prefixes that make a token an escape sequence, sent as it is. |
| `prefix_token` | string | The token for the leader key. |
| `separators` | []string | The characters keys are split on. |
| `max_repeat` | int | The largest repeat send-keys takes. |

### `list-push`

List the phones registered with register-push, and the daemon's VAPID public key. A phone's endpoint is shown only as its origin. Only the person, by the rules of register-push.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The nonce of an attach, or of an attach-presence made with no session, from the same process. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `devices` | object[] | Each phone: device, kinds, service (the endpoint's origin), created, and last_ok or last_error when a push was tried. |
| `vapid_public_key` | string | The daemon's VAPID public key, base64url. A phone needs it before it subscribes. |
| `machine` | string | This machine's name. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-push","params":{"human_nonce":"<from attach-presence>"}}
```

| `host` | string |  |  | A machine from [hosts]: list a pane's queue there, over the link. Session and window name the pane there. The person's replies queued from this machine are by link:THIS-MACHINE there. Only a caller outside every pane may ask; the far machine's policy for this one must allow list. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session listed. |
| `entries` | []object | id, session, window, name, at (Unix nanoseconds), by (human, shell, the queueing pane's window id, or link:HOST), from when given, preview (the first line, at most 80 characters) and state (waiting, delivering or stalled) per entry, next first within each pane. |

### `list-tasks`

List a session's task cards, oldest first.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `stage` | string |  |  | Only the cards in this stage. One of `todo`, `plan`, `doing`, `verify`, `review`, `shipped` or `done`. |
| `holder` | string |  |  | Only the cards this window holds, by id or name. |
| `open` | bool |  | `false` | Leave out the cards that are done. |
| `project` | string |  |  | List one repository's cards, by origin URL, from every session. Takes no session or holder. |
| `hosts` | bool |  | `false` | With project, or with all_sessions: also ask each linked machine for its own cards of the project, or for all its project cards. Rows from another machine carry host; machines that did not answer are in hosts_down. |
| `all_sessions` | bool |  | `false` | List the cards of every session on the daemon, in session name order. Takes no session or holder. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session listed. Absent with all_sessions. |
| `all_sessions` | bool | True when every session was listed. Each row names its session. |
| `tasks` | []object | One row per card: id, session, title, preview (the first line of the prompt, at most 80 characters), stage, stage_at (Unix nanoseconds), holder (window, name, harness, state, held_at, read from the pane now; null when no pane holds it), by (human, shell, the adding pane's window id, link:HOST or plugin:ID), at, blocked (reason, by, at; null when not blocked) and fan (fan, fan_id) for a card added in a fan. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-tasks","params":{"session":"work","open":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-tasks","params":{"all_sessions":true}}
```

### `list-themes`

List the registered themes. Name one to also get its colours as hex and the contrast of each against its own background.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `theme` | string |  |  | Describe this theme as well as listing. Omit to list only. |
| `filter` | string |  |  | Only ids containing this, case-insensitively, e.g. catppuccin. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `themes` | []string | Matching theme ids, capped at 100. truncated reports when the cap applied. |
| `total` | int | How many themes are registered in all. |
| `matched` | int | How many the filter matched, before the cap. |
| `active` | string | The theme this session is set to. With no theme set, it is omarchy where Omarchy is installed, because the client follows Omarchy there. Elsewhere it is empty, which means the terminal's own colours. |
| `active_source` | string | "session" for a theme set on this session, "config" for one config.toml sets, "default" for the built-in. One of `session`, `config` or `default`. |
| `themes_dir` | string | Where a custom theme file goes. Writing <id>.json here registers it. No restart is needed. |
| `problems` | []string | One line per theme file that could not be read, with the reason. Present only when a file is malformed. |
| `palette` | object | Present when theme was given: id, display_name, dark, bg, fg, cursor, swatches (each with hex, ratio, floor, passes) and illegible, the names of the swatches that did not clear their floor. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-themes","params":{"filter":"catppuccin"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-themes","params":{"session":"work","theme":"catppuccin_mocha"}}
```

### `list-windows`

List the windows in a session. The list carries shell, the base name of the shell a new pane in the session runs. Each window carries a host when its process runs on another machine, and omits it when the process is on this one. A window whose shell marks its commands with OSC 133 also carries at_prompt, command_seq, marks_commands (the shell has sent a command-start mark), prompt_marks_only when it ran a command without one, running_cmdline while a command runs, and last_cmdline, last_exit_code and last_duration_ms once one has finished. A pane given grants of its own carries them as grants; a pane on the default of [agents.permissions] omits it. A pane whose process runs on this machine carries pid, the process it started, and tty, its terminal device. A zoomed window carries zoomed, and a floating window carries floating. A window whose program named itself with OSC 1 carries icon_name, the name an unnamed workspace shows while the window is its active one.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `rail` | bool |  | `false` | Also list the rail pane, the pane a client draws in its rail's pane section. It carries rail, the command line it runs. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-windows","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

### `list-workspaces`

List every workspace with its name, how many windows it holds, and which one is showing.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `workspaces` | []object | One row per workspace: workspace, name, window_count, focused_window_id, current, and focus_history, the windows focused there, newest first. |
| `current_workspace` | int | Workspace showing. |
| `order` | []int | Display order, empty when the workspaces are in their plain ascending order. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-workspaces","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

### `list-worktrees`

List the sessions whose directory is a git worktree, with the repository, branch, agent state and fan prompt status of each.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repo` | string |  |  | Only worktrees of this repository, by name. |
| `group` | string |  |  | Only the sessions of this fan-out, by its branch stem. |
| `session` | string |  |  | Only this session's worktree. The close dialog asks for the one it deletes, so the counts under changes run git there and nowhere else. |
| `changes` | bool |  | `false` | Run git in the matching worktrees and report the count of uncommitted changes, the commits ahead of base, and whether the branch is merged. Off by default because it runs git. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `worktrees` | []object | One entry per worktree session: session, repo, repo_root, branch, path, base, group, main (true when the entry is the repository's main checkout, which no worktree verb acts on), managed, gone, state, harness, windows, attached, prompt_status, prompt_note, launched_from (the session whose pane ran the fan, only when a pane did), pr (the branch's pull request as ship-status reports it, only when tuios knows of one), setup (the worktree setup's record, only when it ran one: command, async, state of pending, running, passed or failed, exit, started_at, finished_at, tail with the last four lines, agent of waiting, started or not_started, removed and branch_removed after a failed sync setup, and note), and with changes: changes, ahead, merged, and merged_into. merged is true when the branch has commits of its own and merging it into base, or into the upstream of base, changes nothing. A merge commit, a fast-forward, a rebase and a squash all count. merged_into names the ref, only when merged is true. No fetch is run, so a remote ref is as git last fetched it. |
| `total` | int | How many entries. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-worktrees"}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"list-worktrees","params":{"repo":"api","changes":true}}
```

### `mark-attention`

Snooze an Inbox item, wake it, mark a finished pane unread, restore an item closed a moment ago, or settle an agent, for the person. Only a client attached right now can, with the nonce from its attach reply: an agent cannot hide or reorder what the person reads.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string |  |  | The item. Or name it with session, window and kind. With unsettle: the id settle returned, to put that item back in the Inbox. |
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `kind` | string |  |  | The item's kind, with session and window. One of `approval`, `plan`, `ask`, `question`, `mail`, `errored`, `resume`, `finished`, `outbox` or `held_mail`. |
| `action` | string | yes |  | snooze hides it until a time or until its fact changes, wake shows it again, unread reopens a finished pane's item, restore reopens an item dismissed or snoozed in the last 10 seconds. settle puts the agent in the pane named by session and window on the Settled shelf until it needs the person again, and closes its finished turn. unsettle takes it off the shelf. One of `snooze`, `wake`, `unread`, `restore`, `settle` or `unsettle`. |
| `until` | int |  |  | With snooze: when it opens again, in unix milliseconds. |
| `for_ms` | int |  |  | With snooze: how long, in milliseconds. |
| `until_change` | bool |  | `false` | With snooze: until the pane's state changes. |
| `human_nonce` | string | yes |  | The nonce the daemon issued in an attach reply, for a client attached now over the same kind of connection. The TUI sends its own. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | The item. With settle: the finished turn it closed, empty for none. With unsettle: the item put back, empty for none. |
| `action` | string | What was done. |
| `window` | string | With settle and unsettle: the pane's window id. |
| `snoozed_until` | int | With snooze: when it opens again, in unix nanoseconds, -1 for until it changes. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"mark-attention","params":{"id":"17","action":"snooze","for_ms":900000,"human_nonce":"<from the attach reply>"}}
```

```json
{"id":2,"verb":"mark-attention","params":{"session":"work","window":"review","action":"settle","human_nonce":"<from the attach reply>"}}
```

Write a marker (an m event) into a pane's recording at the current time, so a player can jump to the moment.
