| `summary` | string |  |  | The line the person answers from: the whole request, as the hook reported it. The held item shows it for as long as the hold runs. Nothing is held (reason not_shown) when the Inbox could not show it as it is: longer than 160 bytes, with a control or format character, whitespace it would collapse, or text it would mask. |
| `always_scope` | []string |  |  | What always allows from now on, one rule per line, shown beside the key. Required for always to be offered: one to four lines, each shown as it is. |
| `kind` | string |  | `approval` | What is held: approval for a tool call, plan for a plan an agent in plan mode asks to have approved. One of `approval` or `plan`. |
| `plan` | string |  |  | With kind plan: the plan's text, at most 32 KiB. get-approval serves it while the hold runs. |
| `tool` | string |  |  | The tool the call is for, as the harness names it (Bash, Edit), which the risk rules match on. |
| `target` | string |  |  | What the tool acts on: the command line, or the path. |
| `deny_message` | bool |  | `false` | Whether the harness passes a reason with a deny to its model, so the Inbox may offer to type one. |
| `cwd` | string |  |  | The folder the call runs in, as the harness sent it. The risk rules resolve a relative path against it. Omit it and the pane's own folder is used. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `request_id` | string | The hold's id, empty when nothing was held. |
| `decision` | string | The person's answer, or empty when there is none and the harness should ask in its pane. One of `once`, `always` or `deny`. |
| `message` | string | What the person gave as the reason for a deny, one line. |
| `reason` | string | Why the call returned: answered, or why there is no answer. disabled: the harness is not in [agents.approvals]. not_blocked: the pane is not on needs_input with kind approval. not_shown: the summary could not be shown as it is. viewed: the person has the pane in front of them, or turned to it. timeout, handed_back (enter on the item, or reply-approval ask), superseded (a newer request for the pane), caller_gone, shutdown, and the Inbox close reasons resolved, dismissed, window_closed, session_closed and evicted. |
| `answered_by` | string | The id of the client the answer came from. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"request-approval","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","harness":"claude-code","options":["once","always","deny"],"summary":"approve Bash: go test ./...","always_scope":["Bash(go test:*) in .claude/settings.local.json"]}}
```

### `resize`

Resize a window's PTY.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `width` | int | yes |  | New width in columns. Must be positive. |
| `height` | int | yes |  | New height in rows. Must be positive. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"resize","params":{"session":"work","width":120,"height":40}}
```

### `resize-pane`

Resize a pane this machine is running for another machine. It arrives on its own connection because the pane's connection carries raw bytes and has no room to say anything out of band.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pane` | string |  |  | The pane id open-pane returned. |
| `width` | int |  |  | The new width in cells. |
| `height` | int |  |  | The new height in cells. |
| `xpixel` | int |  |  | The new width in pixels, for programs that scale images to it. Omit to use the fallback cell size. |
| `ypixel` | int |  |  | The new height in pixels. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `pane` | string | The pane that was resized. |
| `width` | int | The width it was set to. |
| `height` | int | The height it was set to. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"resize-pane","params":{"pane":"f2c1","width":120,"height":40}}
```

### `resize-window`

Move one border of a tiled pane. right and down move a border right or down, left and up move it left or up. The border is the one on that side of the pane, or the other one when the pane is at the edge there. Works with no client attached.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Pane to resize. Omit for the caller's own pane, or outside a pane the focused one. |
| `direction` | string | yes |  | The way the border moves. One of `left`, `right`, `up` or `down`. |
| `amount` | int |  | `5` | How far the border moves, in percent of the layout's width or height, from 1 to 50. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | Id of the pane. |
| `changed` | bool | False when no border could move: the pane is alone, floating, or at the smallest or largest share. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"resize-window","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","direction":"right","amount":10}}
```

### `resolve-pane`

Name the pane a process runs in, from its terminal session id and its ancestor pids. It is how a hook reporter finds its pane when the harness or a sandbox wrapper scrubbed TUIOS_PANE_ID from the environment. Only panes on this daemon's own machine are matched.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sid` | int |  |  | The process's session id. Every process whose controlling terminal is a pane shares the id of that pane's shell, so this is tried first. |
| `pids` | []int |  |  | The process's ancestors, nearest first. The first one that is a pane's shell names the pane. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session the pane is in. |
| `window_id` | string | The pane's window id. |
| `by` | string | What matched: tty for the session id, pid for an ancestor. One of `tty` or `pid`. |
| `pid` | int | The shell pid that matched. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"resolve-pane","params":{"sid":4242,"pids":[4250,4243,4242]}}
```

### `respond`

Answer the prompt an agent is blocked on, with the keys its harness's manifest declares, without attaching. It reads the prompt again first and refuses with prompt_changed when the pane left needs_input, when the prompt is not the one prompt_id names, or when another client already answered it: the first answer wins. It then waits up to timeout for the pane to leave needs_input and returns its state. Only the person may call it: a client attached right now passing its attach nonce, or, when the daemon runs with [daemon] respond_from_shell, a process outside every pane. A pane the person gave the respond grant may also call it, for a pane in its own session, in its fan group when it also holds fan, and anywhere when it also holds admin.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | The blocked pane: window id or name. |
| `action` | string |  |  | The answer. approve, approve_always and deny press the keys the rule declares; choose presses an option's number; text types value and submits it. One of `approve`, `approve_always`, `deny`, `choose` or `text`. |
| `value` | string |  |  | The option number for choose, or the answer for text. |
| `prompt_id` | string |  |  | The prompt_id peek-prompt gave. With it, a prompt that changed since the peek is refused rather than answered. Without it, whatever prompt is on the pane now is answered. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The nonce from the attach reply of a client attached now. The Inbox sends its own. |
| `timeout` | int |  | `5000` | Milliseconds to wait for the pane to leave needs_input after the answer, at most 30000. |
| `risk_ack` | []string |  |  | For an approving action on a prompt that matched risk rules: exactly the rules it matched. A deny needs none. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window` | string | The window id. |
| `action` | string | The action taken. |
| `sent` | string | What was pressed, in words: the keys, or text. |
| `prompt_id` | string | The prompt that was answered. |
| `settled_by` | string | How the wait ended: state (the pane left needs_input), prompt (another prompt, or none, is on the screen), gone (the window closed) or timeout. One of `state`, `prompt`, `gone` or `timeout`. |
| `state` | string | The pane's agent state when the wait ended. |
| `message` | string | The pane's agent message when the wait ended. |
| `by_pane` | string | The pane that answered under its respond grant. Absent when the person answered. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"respond","params":{"session":"work","window":"a1b2c3d4","action":"approve","prompt_id":"<from peek-prompt>","human_nonce":"<from the attach reply>"}}
```

### `restore-checkpoint`

Put a pane's git work tree back to a checkpoint. The work tree is first saved as a safety checkpoint, so the restore can be undone by restoring that one. Only files that differ are written or removed. The index, HEAD, the branch and ignored files are not changed. Refused while the pane's agent is working or waiting on a prompt, unless force is given.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `n` | int | yes |  | The checkpoint to restore. |
| `force` | bool |  | `false` | Restore while the agent is mid-turn. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The root of the work tree. |
| `restored` | object | The checkpoint restored. |
| `safety` | object | The checkpoint that holds the work tree as it was before the restore. Restore it to undo. |
| `written` | []string | The files written from the checkpoint. |
| `removed` | []string | The files removed, which the checkpoint does not have. |
| `skipped` | []string | The submodules that differ from the checkpoint. A restore does not change a submodule. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"restore-checkpoint","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","n":1}}
```

### `restrict-connection`

Give up authority on this connection for as long as it is open. scope own reaches only the caller's own session, its fan group and the sessions a fan from it started; read_only refuses every verb that types into a pane. The caller's pane is found from the kernel's record of its pid, and only when that finds none from pane_id and pane_token. A later call may narrow further and never widen. Every verb a restricted connection may not call answers forbidden.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `scope` | string |  | `own` | own restricts every call to the caller's own session and fan group. all leaves the sessions alone, for read_only by itself. One of `own` or `all`. |
| `read_only` | bool |  | `false` | Refuse send-text, send-keys, ask-agent, respond and fan. The caller may still report its own pane's state and meta and leave mail. |
| `pane_id` | string |  |  | The caller's pane, normally $TUIOS_PANE_ID. Used only when the kernel places the caller in no pane, and then only with the matching pane_token. When the kernel places the caller, a different pane_id is refused. |
| `pane_token` | string |  |  | The pane's $TUIOS_PANE_TOKEN, which proves pane_id. It is good for one pane of one daemon start. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `scope` | string | own or all, as the connection now stands. One of `own` or `all`. |
| `read_only` | bool | Whether the connection is now read-only. |
| `window` | string | The caller's pane, empty when it runs in no pane of this daemon. Under scope own such a connection reaches no session. |
| `session` | string | The session of the caller's pane. |
| `via` | string | How the pane was found: pid from the kernel, token from pane_token, empty when it was not. One of `pid` or `token`. |
| `sessions` | []string | Under scope own, the sessions the connection reaches now. Sessions a fan starts later join it. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"restrict-connection","params":{"scope":"own","read_only":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"restrict-connection","params":{"scope":"all","read_only":true}}
```

### `resume-agent`

Resume the agent conversation recorded for a window's pane: type the harness's resume command, built from its manifest's [resume] template and the window's agent_session_id, into the pane's shell. It is how a pane restored after a daemon restart gets its conversation back; the old process does not survive a restart, so this starts a new one on the same conversation. It types only when the pane's own shell holds the terminal's foreground, so the command never lands in a running program, and it closes the pane's resume item in the Inbox. The command is fixed by the manifest and the id is one plain shell token, so the call can type nothing a caller chose.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `dry_run` | bool |  |  | Return the command without typing it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | The window resumed. |
| `harness` | string | The harness the conversation belongs to. |
| `agent_session_id` | string | The conversation resumed. |
| `argv` | []string | The command, one token per element. |
| `command` | string | The command as typed. |
| `typed` | bool | Whether it was typed: false for dry_run. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"resume-agent","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"resume-agent","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","dry_run":true}}
```

### `retry-host`

Dial the link to one host again now. The backoff starts again from one second. If the link is up, the link pings the host instead. If the host does not answer in 5 seconds, the link dials again. If the host waits for a Tailscale SSH sign-in, the link dials every few seconds for two minutes. A dial in progress continues.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `host` | string |  |  | A host by name from the [hosts] config table. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `host` | string | The host. |
| `status` | string | The link state when the call was made. |
| `approval_url` | string | The Tailscale sign-in page, when the link waits for one and has it. Omitted otherwise. |
| `approval_refused` | bool | True when the link waits for a sign-in and the banner named an address that is not a Tailscale login origin. Omitted otherwise. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"retry-host","params":{"host":"build"}}
```

### `review-diff`

Read what the agent in a pane changed: the diff of its worktree against the base it was made from, or for a plain repository against the upstream merge base, untracked files included (one larger than max_untracked_mb is listed as skipped and not read) and ignored files left out. Nothing in the repository, its index or its working tree is changed. The text is the repository's, so it is marked untrusted.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `base` | string |  |  | The ref to diff against. Omit for the worktree's base, else the upstream merge base, else HEAD (uncommitted changes only). |
| `against` | string |  |  | A fan sibling session to diff against instead of a base: the two attempts compared with each other. |
| `uncommitted` | bool |  | `false` | Only what is not committed yet, against HEAD. |
| `paths` | []string |  |  | Only these paths, relative to the repository root. Omit for every changed file. |
| `context` | int |  | `3` | Lines of context around each change, 0 to 20. |
| `index` | string |  |  | Read the worktree's index instead of the base. unstaged is the index against the working files, untracked files included. staged is HEAD against the index. Omit for the diff against the base. Not with base, against or uncommitted. One of `unstaged` or `staged`. |
| `highlight` | bool |  | `false` | Add the syntax colour and the changed part to each line: hl and changed. classes names the colour classes. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane reviewed. |
| `window` | string | The pane reviewed, by id: the one its notes are kept for. |
| `repo_root` | string | The repository's main checkout. |
| `worktree` | string | The worktree the diff was read from, symbolic links resolved. |
| `base` | string | The base as named (the ref given, the worktree's base, the upstream branch, or HEAD), empty with against. With index unstaged it is index, with index staged it is HEAD. |
| `base_sha` | string | The commit the diff runs from: the merge base of HEAD and the base, or HEAD for uncommitted changes only. With index it is a tree: the index, or HEAD's tree. |
| `index` | string | The index mode read, unstaged or staged. Absent without index. |
| `classes` | []string | With highlight: the names of the colour classes, in the order of their numbers in hl. Absent without highlight. |
| `uncommitted` | bool | The diff shows only what is not committed: asked for, or no base was found. |
| `tree_sha` | string | The tree the working state was written to, for a later diff of the same state. |
| `against` | string | The sibling session, when one was given. |
| `against_tree` | string | The tree the sibling's working state was written to, with against. |
| `files` | []object | One entry per changed file: path, old_path, status (A, M, D, R, or U for untracked), added, removed, binary, truncated, and hunks, each with header, old_start, old_lines, new_start, new_lines and lines of op (context, add or delete), old, new, text and no_newline. With highlight a line also has hl, its colour as [start, end, class] runs in bytes of text, and changed, the [start, end] bytes that changed against its paired line. Each is absent when empty. |
| `totals` | object | files, added and removed over the whole diff. |
| `truncated` | bool | The diff passed a cap (400 files, 2 MiB, 5000 lines in a file) and the files past it carry counts only. |
| `notes` | []object | The pane's review notes, found again in this diff, as review-note lists them. |
| `untrusted` | bool | Always true: the text is the repository's, not the daemon's. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-diff","params":{"session":"api-fan-retry-2"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-diff","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","uncommitted":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-diff","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","index":"unstaged"}}
```

### `review-note`

Keep the review notes on a pane's changes: add one on a line or a hunk, edit, remove, list or clear them. Notes are held by the daemon per worktree, so every client and the CLI see the same ones, and follow their line as the diff moves. A note is the person's only with a live human_nonce, and a pane without admin adds or edits notes only on panes it could type into.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | string | yes |  | What to do. One of `add`, `edit`, `remove`, `list` or `clear`. |
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `path` | string |  |  | The file the note is on, relative to the repository root. Needed to add. |
| `line` | int |  |  | The line the note is on, on side. Omit with hunk for a note on the whole hunk. |
| `side` | string |  | `new` | Which side of the diff line counts on. One of `new` or `old`. |
| `quote` | string |  |  | The text of the line, which finds the line again when the diff moves. |
| `hunk` | string |  |  | The hunk header the note is on. |
| `text` | string |  |  | The note, at most 1000 bytes. Needed to add and edit. |
| `id` | string |  |  | The note to edit or remove. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The nonce from the attach reply of a client attached now. The TUI sends its own. Without a live one the call is not the person's. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane the notes are for, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The worktree the notes are kept on. |
| `id` | string | The note added or edited. |
| `removed` | int | How many notes remove or clear dropped. |
| `kept` | int | clear only: how many of the pane's notes were left because this caller may not change them (the person's, or another pane's or machine's). |
| `notes` | []object | The pane's notes after the call, by path then line: id, path, side, line, quote, hunk_header, text, by, at, sent_at and outdated. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-note","params":{"action":"add","session":"work","window":"build","path":"api/retry.go","line":42,"quote":"if err == nil {","text":"log the attempt number here too"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-note","params":{"action":"list","session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

### `review-stage`

Stage or unstage a change in the worktree of a pane. Name a whole file, one hunk, or some lines of a hunk. Only the index changes. The working files do not change. Every result carries an undo object. Send it back with action undo to undo the call. The undo result carries its own undo object, which redoes the call.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | string | yes |  | What to do. One of `stage`, `unstage` or `undo`. |
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `path` | string |  |  | The file, relative to the repository root. Needed to stage and unstage. |
| `hunk` | string |  |  | The hunk header, exactly as review-diff returned it. Stage reads it from index unstaged, unstage from index staged. Omit for the whole file. |
| `lines` | []int |  |  | Indices into the lines of the hunk. Only added and removed lines count. Context lines are ignored. Omit for the whole hunk. |
| `context` | int |  | `3` | The context of the diff the hunk came from, 0 to 20. It must match that diff. |
| `undo` | object |  |  | For action undo: the undo object of the result to undo, with entries and after. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The nonce from the attach reply of a client attached now. The TUI sends its own. Without a live one the call is not the person's. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The worktree whose index changed. |
| `path` | string | The file named. |
| `action` | string | The action done: stage, unstage or undo. |
| `undo` | object | entries: the index entries of the paths touched before the call, each path with mode and sha, or absent. after: the same paths after the call. Send it with action undo to undo the call. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-stage","params":{"action":"stage","session":"work","window":"build","path":"api/retry.go","hunk":"@@ -40,7 +40,9 @@ func Do(ctx context.Context","lines":[3,4]}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-stage","params":{"action":"unstage","session":"work","window":"build","path":"api/retry.go"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"review-stage","params":{"action":"undo","session":"work","window":"build","undo":{"entries":[{"path":"api/retry.go","mode":"100644","sha":"<sha>"}],"after":[{"path":"api/retry.go","mode":"100644","sha":"<sha>"}]}}}
```

### `run`

Type one command line at a pane's shell prompt, wait for the shell to report it finished, and return its exit code and output. Needs a shell that marks its commands with OSC 133, and refuses with no_shell_integration one that marks only its prompts; refuses with not_at_prompt when a command, or another run, is already running there. From a pane, window is required.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `command` | string |  |  | The command line, typed as a paste and submitted with Enter. One line, no control characters: join several commands with ; or &&. |
| `timeout` | int |  | `30000` | Milliseconds to wait for the command to finish before failing with the timeout code. The command keeps running after a timeout. |
| `lines` | int |  |  | Keep only the last N lines of the output. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `exit_code` | int | The status the shell reported. Omitted when the shell sent none, which a bash integration does on ctrl+c. |
| `output` | string | What the command printed, as plain text, read from the pane between the shell's marks. At most the last 256 KiB. |
| `truncated` | bool | True when the output was cut, or its start had already left the scrollback. |
| `cmdline` | string | The command line as the shell showed it, cut to 512 bytes with likely secrets masked. |
| `duration_ms` | int | How long the command ran, from the shell's marks. |
| `command_seq` | int | How many commands the pane has finished, this one included. |
| `window` | string | Id of the pane the command ran in. |
| `session` | string | The session the pane is in. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"run","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","command":"go test ./...","timeout":600000}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"run","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","command":"make lint","lines":40}}
```

### `run-command`

Run one tape command, or any keybinding action by the name config.toml binds it to. Prefer a verb where one exists: a verb reports what changed, this reports only that the command ran.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `command` | string |  |  | Tape command name, e.g. "ToggleZoom" or "SnapLeft", or a keybinding action name, e.g. "toggle_spotlight" (tuios keybinds list prints them). An action runs on an attached client. Only a user action (user:NAME) takes args, each key=value. |
| `args` | []string |  |  | Arguments for the command. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `command` | string | The command that ran. |
| `routed` | bool | True when an attached client ran it, false when the daemon did. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"run-command","params":{"session":"work","command":"ToggleZoom"}}
```

### `run-task-verify`

Run a board's verify command in the worktree of a pane here, for the card's owner. Only over a link.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `window` | string | yes |  | The pane. |
| `command` | string |  |  | The shell line. |
| `probe` | bool |  | `false` | Only say if the pane is here. |
| `timeout_ms` | int |  | `600000` | The limit. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `here` | bool | With probe: the pane is here. |
| `exit` | int | The exit status. |
| `note` | string | Why it did not end. |
| `tail` | string | The end of the output. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"run-task-verify","params":{"window":"a1b2c3d4","command":"go test ./...","timeout_ms":600000}}
```

### `screenshot`

Render a window to an image file.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `format` | string |  | `png` | Output format. One of `png`, `svg`, `ansi`, `html` or `txt`. |
| `frame` | string |  | `window` | Dressing around the capture. One of `window`, `plain` or `none`. |
| `theme` | string |  |  | Render in this theme instead of the session's. Truecolor cells are unchanged by it. |
| `scrollback` | bool |  | `false` | Put the pane's history above the screen in the picture. |
| `lines` | int |  |  | Bound the history rows to the last N. Needs scrollback. |
| `cursor` | bool |  | `false` | Draw the cursor cell. |
| `out` | string |  |  | Write here instead of generating a name under screenshot.directory. A linked machine cannot set it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The file that was written. |
| `host` | string | The machine the path is on: daemon or client. |
| `format` | string | The format that was rendered. |
| `cols` | int | Grid width in cells. |
| `rows` | int | Grid height in cells, history included. |
| `bytes` | int | Size of the written file. |
| `warnings` | []string | Anything the render had to guess or fall back on. Empty when there is nothing to say. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"screenshot","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","format":"svg"}}
```

### `search-panes`

Find text in the history and the screen of panes on this machine. The newest match in a pane comes first, and the pane with the newest output comes first. Each pane gets 50 ms. A pane that takes longer is cut off, and truncated is then true.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `query` | string | yes |  | The text to find, at most 512 bytes. It is literal text unless regex is true. |
| `regex` | bool |  | `false` | Read query as a Go regular expression. A pattern that matches an empty line is refused. |
| `case` | string |  | `smart` | How letter case counts. smart ignores case unless the query has a capital letter. One of `smart`, `sensitive` or `insensitive`. |
| `sessions` | []string |  |  | The sessions to search. Omit to search every session. |
| `max` | int |  | `200` | The most matches to return, at most 1000. |
| `per_pane` | int |  | `20` | The most matches to return from one pane, at most 200. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `matches` | []object | One entry per matched row: session, window (the window id), name, workspace, row, text, start, end and age_rows. row counts history rows from the top, then screen rows. text is the row, cut to 400 characters around the match. start and end are the first match's character offsets in text. age_rows is how many rows the row is above the bottom of the screen. |
| `panes_searched` | int | How many panes were read. |
| `truncated` | bool | True when more matches exist than came back: max or per_pane was reached, or a pane took longer than 50 ms. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"search-panes","params":{"query":"error"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"search-panes","params":{"query":"panic: .*nil","regex":true,"sessions":["work"],"max":50}}
```

### `select-workspace`

Show a workspace. To rename or reorder workspaces, use set-workspace-name and set-workspace-order.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `workspace` | int | yes |  | Workspace number to show. |
| `return_to` | int |  | `0` | Workspace to show when this workspace loses its last pane, before any other. tuios xpanes sets it to the workspace that ran it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `current_workspace` | int | Workspace now showing. |
| `focused_window_id` | string | Window focused on it, empty when it holds none. |
| `window_count` | int | How many windows it holds. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"select-workspace","params":{"session":"work","workspace":2}}
```

### `send-agent-message`

Leave a message in a session's agent ring, addressed to one window's inbox or, with no recipient, to the session as a notice. With select and confirm it goes to every agent pane a selector matches, each in its own session's ring. It queues rather than typing, so it is safe to send to an agent that is mid-turn.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `to` | string |  |  | Recipient window id or name, or human for the person at the attached client. Omit to post a notice everyone in the session can read. |
| `from` | string |  |  | The sending window, normally $TUIOS_PANE_ID. It is a claim the daemon cannot verify, and it is what the rate cap and the loop guards are keyed on. From another machine it is kept as a label and not resolved. human from a process inside a pane of this daemon is refused with forbidden. |
| `from_host` | string |  |  | The name of the machine the sender is on, normally $TUIOS_HOST. Kept only for a send that arrived over a link, as the sender's own claim. |
| `subject` | string |  |  | Optional one-line summary, at most 120 characters. |
| `text` | string |  |  | The message body, at most 8 KiB. Required without parts. With parts it is ignored. |
| `parts` | []object |  |  | The message in ordered parts, as queue-prompt takes them: text, paste and image. When the whole message fits 8 KiB and holds no image, the blocks are put in their place. Otherwise each paste and image is written to the paste folder on this machine, kept for 24 hours, attached to the message, and named in the body: [Pasted text #1: PATH, 42 lines], [Image #1: PATH]. The attachments, given and written, are at most 8. From a linked machine, writing a file needs files in its policy. |
| `reply_to` | int |  |  | The id of the message this one answers. The reply joins that message's thread, and a reply to a reply joins the same one. A reply is the only acknowledgement between agents that means anything. |
| `attachments` | []string |  |  | Absolute paths to existing files on the daemon's host. The ring stores the reference, never the bytes, so the producer keeps the file. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The nonce from an attach reply, which the tuios client sends with a reply from its mail overlay. A message from human is stored as verified_human only when this matches a client attached to the session now, over the same kind of connection, the sender is outside every pane, and, where the kernel gives both pids, the sender is the process that attached. Without it, from human is stored as claimed_human. |
| `host` | string |  |  | A machine in the [hosts] table: deliver to session there over this machine's link, and keep the message here while the link is down or the far machine does not answer, to deliver in order. The answer is then the far machine's, with host, or queued with queue_id. session is required. from human arrives there as claimed_human. Not taken over a link, or with select. |
| `select` | string |  |  | A selector: space-separated key:value terms, all of which must match, each with comma-separated alternatives. Keys: harness (id or program name), state, needs:you, session (glob), group (fan-out group, glob), host (local or a host name, glob), name (window name, glob), cwd (the directory or under it; ~ is home), task (a task card the pane holds, by id, glob), stage (the stage of a card the pane holds), parent and root (the parent agent pane, or the first of its chain, by window id or a start of it). Sends one directed message to every pane the selector matches, at most 32. It reaches agent panes on this machine, in every session, and takes no session and no window. Without confirm nothing is sent: the call fails with confirm_required, whose hint lists the panes in available and carries the token in confirm. |
| `confirm` | string |  |  | The token for the set of panes the selector matches, from a confirm_required hint or from list-agents with the same selector. The write goes ahead only when the selector still matches exactly that set; otherwise it fails with confirm_required again and the new set. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `results` | []object | With select: one row per pane, in session order: session, window, name, ok, and message_id and thread_id when it was sent or error (code, message, hint) when it was not. Each pane goes through the checks a single send makes, the rate cap included. |
| `sent` | int | With select: how many panes the message reached. |
| `failed` | int | With select: how many panes refused it. |
| `message_id` | int | The id of the stored message. |
| `queued` | bool | With host: true when the message waits here: the link was down, the far machine did not answer, or earlier mail for it still waits. |
| `queue_id` | int | With queued: the message's place in this machine's outbox. |
| `waiting` | int | With queued: how many messages now wait for that machine. |
| `kind` | string | message for a directed message, notice for a session-wide one. One of `message` or `notice`. |
| `to` | string | The resolved recipient window id, empty for a notice. |
| `to_name` | string | The recipient's name at the time of sending. |
| `from` | string | The resolved sender window id. |
| `sent_at` | int | Unix-nano time the message was stored. |
| `reply_to` | int | The message this one answers, zero when it answers nothing. |
| `thread_id` | int | The thread this message belongs to, which is the id of the message the thread started from. A message that starts a thread carries its own id. Pass it to the read and wait filters. |
| `reply_to_missing` | bool | The message being answered had already been dropped from the ring, so the thread is rooted on the id the reply named rather than on the parent's own thread. The reply still stands. |
| `origin` | string | link when the send arrived from another machine over the daemon's link, empty when it came from this machine. The daemon decides it from the connection, never from the request. |
| `origin_host` | string | The machine name the sender claimed, for a send from another machine. |
| `verified_human` | bool | True for a message from human that carried the nonce of a client attached to the session now. |
| `claimed_human` | bool | True for a message from human that carried no such nonce. It is a claim anything with the socket can make. |
| `held` | bool | True when this machine's link policy (hold_mail) put a message from another machine in the person's Inbox instead of the recipient's. The person passes it on with release-agent-message. |
| `held_for` | string | With held, the window the message was addressed to, empty for a notice. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-agent-message","params":{"session":"work","to":"build","from":"$TUIOS_PANE_ID","subject":"tests green","text":"the suite passes on my branch"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-agent-message","params":{"session":"work","text":"deploying in five minutes"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-agent-message","params":{"session":"work","to":"review","text":"here is the flame graph","attachments":["/tmp/flame.png"]}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-agent-message","params":{"session":"work","to":"build","from":"$TUIOS_PANE_ID","reply_to":12,"text":"retested, still green"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-agent-message","params":{"select":"group:fan/add-retry","text":"main moved, rebase before you push"}}
```

### `send-keys`

Send keys to a window's program: Up, PageDown, ctrl+c. With a window they go to that window's terminal; without one, to an attached client (the window manager) or else the focused window. From a pane, window is required.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `keys` | string |  |  | Keys split on spaces and commas, e.g. "Down Down PageDown" or "ctrl+c". A key is a name (Enter Tab BTab Space Comma Escape Backspace Up Down Right Left Home End PageUp PageDown Insert Delete F1-F12, case-insensitive, also as arrow-up, KEY_UP, <Up>, PgDn), one character, a name or character after ctrl+, alt+ or shift+ (or tmux C-, M-, S-), an escape sequence written \e[A, or PREFIX for the leader key. list-keys returns the full list. |
| `literal` | bool |  | `false` | Send the keys to the PTY without parsing them as key names. |
| `raw` | bool |  | `false` | Treat every character as its own key instead of splitting on spaces and commas. |
| `repeat` | int |  | `1` | Send the whole sequence this many times, 1 to 1000. |
| `allow_blocked` | bool |  | `false` | From a pane, type into a pane on needs_input instead of refusing with agent_blocked. What is typed then answers its prompt, which also needs the respond grant. The person, outside every pane, is not held. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `sent_to` | string | window when the keys were written to a window's terminal, client when an attached client took them as the person's keys. |
| `window_id` | string | The window the keys were written to, when sent_to is window. |
| `window` | string | That window's name, or its title when it has no name. |
| `keys` | int | How many keys were sent, counting repeats. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-keys","params":{"session":"work","window":"review","keys":"Down","repeat":5}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-keys","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","keys":"ctrl+c"}}
```

### `send-review`

Send a pane's unsent review notes to its agent as one message, through the delivery queue: typed now when the agent is at rest, else when it next comes to rest. The message says it is from the person only with a live human_nonce, and labels each note written by someone other than the sender with its author. It types into the pane, so a pane may send only to a pane that holds nothing it does not, and a note whose author may not type there now is withheld.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `ids` | []string |  |  | Only these notes. Omit for every unsent one. |
| `now` | bool |  | `false` | Type it now or not at all: refuse with agent_blocked or not_ready instead of queueing. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The nonce from the attach reply of a client attached now. The TUI sends its own. Without a live one the call is not the person's. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `notes` | int | How many notes the message carries. |
| `ids` | []string | The notes sent, now marked with sent_at. |
| `queued_id` | string | The queue entry, which cancel-queued takes. |
| `position` | int | Its place in the pane's queue, 1 for next. |
| `queued` | int | How many messages the pane's queue holds now. |
| `delivering` | bool | The message is next and the agent is at rest, so it is typed within about a second. |
| `withheld` | []string | Notes left out, not sent and not marked: their author (a pane, or a linked machine) may not type into this pane now, or the pane that wrote them is gone. Absent when none. |
| `withheld_reason` | string | Why the first withheld note was left out. Absent when none. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-review","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","human_nonce":"<from the attach reply>"}}
```

### `send-text`

Send literal text to a window's PTY. From a pane, window is required.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `text` | string |  |  | Text written verbatim to the PTY. |
| `paste` | bool |  | `false` | Send the text as a paste. Control characters other than tab, line feed and carriage return are removed. The text is wrapped in the bracketed paste delimiters when the program in the pane turned bracketed paste on. |
| `submit` | bool |  | `false` | Paste the text and submit it with the Enter key of the harness in the pane, after a short wait for the paste to be taken in, as ask-agent does. |
| `allow_blocked` | bool |  | `false` | From a pane, type into a pane on needs_input instead of refusing with agent_blocked. What is typed then answers its prompt, which also needs the respond grant. The person, outside every pane, is not held. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-text","params":{"session":"work","text":"echo hi\n"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-text","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","text":"line one\nline two","paste":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"send-text","params":{"session":"work","text":"Fix the test\nthen commit","submit":true}}
```

### `session-info`

Report details about one session.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"session-info","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

### `set-agent-meta`

Record display metadata about the agent in a window's pane (model, context used, cost, a short summary). The rail draws it under the agent's row. It is display only: it never changes the agent state, a wait, an alert or a message. Keys keep the position they first arrived in. The metadata clears when the agent leaves the pane. A call that sets keys to the values they hold changes nothing and pushes nothing to clients; a TTL is renewed only once less than half of it remains. The keys now and prompt are written by tuios from hook activity and are refused here.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `tokens` | object |  |  | Key to value. A string sets the key, null removes it. At most 16 keys per call and 32 per pane. A key is 1 to 24 lower-case letters, digits, '_' or '-', starting with a letter, and not now, prompt or subagents. A value has control characters replaced and is cut to 80 characters. Required unless clear is true. |
| `source` | string |  |  | Who is writing, recorded on each key so clear can remove only this writer's keys. |
| `ttl_ms` | int |  | `0` | Milliseconds the keys set by this call live before the daemon drops them. 0 keeps them until they are removed or the agent leaves. At most one day. |
| `clear` | bool |  | `false` | Remove every key this source wrote, or every key when source is empty, before applying tokens. The keys tuios writes, now, prompt and subagents, stay. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | The window the metadata was recorded on. |
| `meta` | object | The keys the pane holds after the call, key to value. |
| `truncated` | []string | The keys whose values were cut to the length limit. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-meta","params":{"session":"work","source":"statusline","tokens":{"model":"opus","context":"42%"},"ttl_ms":60000}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-meta","params":{"session":"work","tokens":{"summary":null}}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-meta","params":{"session":"work","source":"statusline","clear":true}}
```

### `set-agent-quiet`

Mark a window quiet, or clear the mark. A quiet window opens no finished, question or errored item in the Inbox, and its open items of those kinds close. A pane that calls this leaves an open question open. Its agent state and agent-state events do not change, and an approval or a login still reaches the person. The window must run an agent. Needs the admin grant.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `quiet` | bool |  | `true` | True marks the window quiet, and false clears the mark. Clearing it opens no old item, except a question the pane waits on now. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session that holds the window. |
| `window_id` | string | The window that was marked. |
| `quiet` | bool | Whether the window is now quiet. |
| `closed` | int | How many open Inbox items this call closed. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-quiet","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-quiet","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","quiet":false}}
```

### `set-agent-session`

Store the conversation id a harness reports for a window's pane, without changing the pane's agent state, its source or its harness attribution. It is what a hook sends for a harness whose hooks are trusted to name the conversation but not to report its state. The id is stored and persisted as the window's agent_session_id, the same field set-agent-state's agent_session_id writes.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `harness` | string |  |  | Id of the harness the conversation belongs to. A window attributed to a different harness refuses the report with reason foreign_harness. |
| `agent_session_id` | string |  |  | The harness's own id for the conversation, at most 256 bytes. |
| `transcript_path` | string |  |  | The exact transcript file, held to set-agent-state's same-machine, own-pane, regular-file and manifest-folder rules. A transcript update never replaces a different conversation, even at rest. |
| `transcript_leaf` | string |  |  | Pi's selected entry, as set-agent-state describes it. An empty string selects an empty branch. Updating selection changes no agent state and raises a transcript event even without a file append. |
| `harness_pid` | int |  |  | The pid of the harness process that ran the hook. While the window is working or needs_input, a different id from a different process than the one that reported the stored id is a nested run and is refused with reason foreign_session. A harness whose environment names this pane in TUIOS_PANE_ID but that runs outside it, neither the pane's process nor on its terminal, with a harness daemon among its ancestors, even one the pane started (Claude Code's daemon or the pty host of its background sessions), is refused with reason foreign_pane: a background session that inherited the variable. A harness under anything else that left the pane, such as a tmux server or a rootless container's conmon, is taken for the pane. Kept in daemon memory only. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `agent_session_id` | string | The id the window holds after the call. |
| `transcript_refused` | string | Why the transcript update was refused. Absent when accepted or not provided. |
| `applied` | bool | Whether the report was taken. A report naming the id the window already holds is taken and changes nothing. |
| `reason` | string | Why the report was not taken. Absent when it was. One of `foreign_session`, `foreign_harness` or `foreign_pane`. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-session","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","harness":"qwen","agent_session_id":"5f1c","harness_pid":4100}}
```

### `set-agent-state`

Set the agent state a window's pane reports (working, needs_input, idle, done, errored, or none to clear). A pane reports its own state by calling this against the daemon socket.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `state` | string |  |  | The agent state to record. One of `none`, `working`, `needs_input`, `idle`, `done`, `errored`, `unknown` or `limit_reached`. |
| `message` | string |  |  | Optional short note reported with the state, e.g. what the agent is waiting for. |
| `source` | string |  | `report` | Where the state came from. A source ranked below the one that last set the window is refused. The result then reports applied false and the state that stands. One of `report`, `osc`, `screen` or `stall`. |
| `harness` | string |  |  | Optional id of the harness the state is about, reported back by get-agent-state. |
| `kind` | string |  |  | What a needs_input state waits for: approval for a tool call waiting to be allowed, question for anything else. Only valid with needs_input, except usage_limit, the older way to say limit_reached: with errored or limit_reached, the report lands as limit_reached, which stops task dispatch. Omitted, it is guessed from message. Reported back by get-agent-state and list-agents as blocked_by. One of `approval`, `question`, `auth` or `usage_limit`. |
| `agent_session_id` | string |  |  | The harness's own id for the conversation, as a hook reports it. It is stored on the window for a later resume. It also turns on the nested-session guard: while the pane's harness is working or needs_input by its own report, a report for a different conversation or from a different harness is refused with reason foreign_session or foreign_harness. |
| `transcript_path` | string |  |  | The transcript file the harness is writing, as a hook reports it. For a harness whose manifest has a transcript reader, the window is joined to this exact file instead of a searched one. It must be a regular file under the harness's transcript folder, and a process in a pane may name one only for its own pane. Kept in daemon memory only. |
| `transcript_leaf` | string |  |  | Pi's selected tree entry id, at most 128 letters, digits, '-' or '_'. A present empty string selects the empty branch; omission preserves the previous selection or uses the last complete entry for an old hook. Requires harness pi and transcript_path. Kept in daemon memory only. |
| `if_state` | string |  |  | Comma-separated states. The report applies only when the window is in one of them now, and is otherwise refused with reason if_state. One of `none`, `working`, `needs_input`, `idle`, `done`, `errored`, `unknown` or `limit_reached`. |
| `subagent_id` | string |  |  | With needs_input: the id of the subagent whose prompt the pane shows, as the harness's hook names it. Omitted, the prompt is the pane's own agent's. Kept in daemon memory only. |
| `if_subagent_id` | string |  |  | The report applies only while the window is on a needs_input that named this subagent_id, and is otherwise refused with reason if_state. A subagent's hook clears the prompt it raised with it, and leaves one the main agent or another subagent raised. |
| `notification` | bool |  |  | The report comes from an event the harness sends once and never takes back, such as Claude Code's Notification and PermissionRequest hooks. Its state holds until the pane writes again, and from then on a look at the pane's title or screen may replace it, as for a desktop notification the pane sends. A needs_input marked so ends on a look only while the screen reads idle, an empty prompt box, and only once that reading has held as long as a working pane's idle must; neither the title's idle nor any working look ends it, and a hook's report ends it at once. |
| `harness_pid` | int |  |  | The pid of the harness process that ran the hook. With agent_session_id, a different session from the same harness process is a new conversation in that process (/clear or /resume, even after an interrupted turn that never reported Stop), so it takes the pane over instead of being refused as foreign_session. A harness whose environment names this pane in TUIOS_PANE_ID but that runs outside it, neither the pane's process nor on its terminal, with a harness daemon among its ancestors, even one the pane started (Claude Code's daemon or the pty host of its background sessions), is refused with reason foreign_pane: a background session that inherited the variable. A harness under anything else that left the pane, such as a tmux server or a rootless container's conmon, is taken for the pane. A harness_pid that is not the caller or one of its ancestors is not believed, and the caller is checked in its place. Kept in daemon memory only. |
| `session_start` | bool |  |  | Marks the report of a conversation starting or resuming, as a SessionStart hook sends it. For a harness whose manifest sets shared_server (Codex), a report for a conversation other than the pane's own takes the pane over only with this set, and is otherwise refused with reason foreign_session. |
| `agent_cwd` | string |  |  | The folder the harness reported for its session (a hook payload's cwd). The first one for a session is kept as the agent's start folder, when it is the folder the pane's process runs in. The risk rules use it as the root of a pane that started in the home folder or above. Later reports never move it. |
| `activity` | object |  |  | One hook event of the pane's own agent, for its activity ring: event (prompt, tool, tool_done, tool_failed or turn_end), tool, target, text, files, ok and model. It is recorded whenever the report passes the identity guard, whether or not the state applies, and read back with agent-activity. It also sets the reserved metadata keys now (a tool call starting) and prompt (a prompt), and model when the harness named one. Display only. A hook sends it only to a daemon whose list-verbs lists it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `state` | string | The state the window shows after the call, which is the reported one only when applied is true. |
| `applied` | bool | Whether this report set the state. |
| `reason` | string | Why the report was not applied. Absent when it was. One of `outranked`, `if_state`, `foreign_session`, `foreign_harness` or `foreign_pane`. |
| `activity_recorded` | bool | With activity: whether it went into the pane's activity ring. False for a report the identity guard refused. Absent without activity. |
| `transcript_refused` | string | Why the window was not joined to transcript_path. Absent when it was, or when no path was given. |
| `session` | string | The session of the window the report went to, when the daemon placed it on another window. Absent otherwise. |
| `window` | string | The window the report went to, when the window named is gone and the report is a shared_server harness's (Codex) from outside every pane. The daemon places it on the one live pane that runs its conversation: the pane that holds agent_session_id, the pane joined to transcript_path, or the pane whose agent started near the start of that transcript, in its folder. Absent otherwise. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-state","params":{"session":"work","state":"needs_input","message":"awaiting approval"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-state","params":{"session":"work","state":"working","source":"osc","harness":"claude-code"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-agent-state","params":{"session":"work","state":"needs_input","kind":"approval","harness":"claude-code","agent_session_id":"5f1c","message":"approve Bash: go test ./..."}}
```

### `set-buffer`

Store content in a paste buffer and put it on top. With no name a new buffer is made, append or not, as tmux does; a name makes the buffer a named one. Empty content stores nothing and is no error. When the buffers tuios named pass the limit, the oldest of them go; past the byte cap the oldest go whatever their name. A set from outside every pane, or from a pane with admin, makes the person's buffer; a set from a pane without admin makes that pane's own. From a pane this needs the write grant, and a pane without admin may set only its own buffers, or make a new one with no name.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | string |  |  | The content as text. Give data or data_b64. It may not be empty or larger than the byte cap. |
| `data_b64` | string |  |  | The content as base64, for any bytes. It wins over data. |
| `upload` | string |  |  | An id that sends the content in parts, each in its own call on one connection. The buffer is set once, when the part without more arrives. |
| `more` | bool |  | `false` | More parts of this upload follow. |
| `name` | string |  |  | The buffer to set: 1 to 64 printable characters. Omit for a new buffer. |
| `append` | bool |  | `false` | Add the text to the end of the buffer instead of replacing it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | The buffer's name: bufferN for one tuios named, or the name set-buffer gave it. |
| `bytes` | int | How many bytes the buffer holds. |
| `created` | int | Unix-nano time the content was last set. |
| `version` | int | A number that changes each time the content is set. Pass it back as version to act only on this content. |
| `automatic` | bool | True when tuios named the buffer. |
| `sample` | string | The start of the content on one line, with control characters and bytes that are not UTF-8 shown as escapes. It never cuts a character. Print it as it is. |
| `pane` | string | The pane that owns the buffer, by its window name, or its short id once it is gone. Absent for the person's buffers. |
| `stored` | bool | False when the content was empty and nothing was stored. The other fields are then absent. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-buffer","params":{"data":"make test"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-buffer","params":{"name":"deploy","data":"kubectl rollout restart deploy/api"}}
```

### `set-fan-verdict`

Record a judge's pick among the attempts of a fan: the attempt it recommends, the verdict, its reasons and notes on each attempt. Only the judge pane judge-fan started for the fan, or a call from outside every pane, records one: a pane of an attempt is refused. The daemon records which of the two it was as by. It is written on every attempt, and compare-fan and the agents view show it. A later call replaces it. Nothing is kept or removed: keep-fan does that.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | yes |  | Any session of the fan. |
| `pick` | string | yes |  | The session of the attempt the judge recommends. |
| `summary` | string | yes |  | The verdict in a sentence or two, at most 400 bytes. |
| `reasons` | []string |  |  | The reasons, one sentence each, at most 6. |
| `notes` | object |  |  | Notes per attempt: session name to a list of sentences, at most 4 each. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `group` | string | The fan's group. |
| `pick` | string | The attempt picked. |
| `by` | string | judge for the judge's pane, you for a call from outside every pane. |
| `attempts` | []string | Every attempt the verdict was written on. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-fan-verdict","params":{"session":"api-fan-retry","pick":"api-fan-retry-2","summary":"Keep B. It is the smallest change and passes the tests.","reasons":["A reads hits without the lock."],"notes":{"api-fan-retry":["Adds a test for eviction."]}}}
```

### `set-keep-awake`

Set [daemon] keep_awake: off, on (while the daemon runs) or agents (while an agent pane on this machine works). It applies at once and is saved to config.toml, so the settings page, the file and every client show one value.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | string | yes |  | The new mode. One of `off`, `agents` or `on`. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `mode` | string | [daemon] keep_awake in force. |
| `held` | bool | True while the daemon holds the machine awake. |
| `working` | int | Agent panes that work now on this machine. |
| `method` | string | How the hold is made. |
| `error` | string | Why the machine could not be held awake. Absent when nothing failed. |
| `saved` | bool | True when config.toml now holds the mode. |
| `save_error` | string | Why config.toml could not be written. The mode applies until the daemon stops. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-keep-awake","params":{"mode":"agents"}}
```

### `set-layout`

Turn tiling on or off, tidy the splits, and shape the master-stack layout. Needs an attached client.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `tiling` | bool |  |  | Tile the panes automatically, or let them float. |
| `equalize` | bool |  | `false` | Reset every split ratio so the panes share the space evenly. |
| `rotate` | bool |  | `false` | Flip the axis of the split holding the focused pane. |
| `master_position` | string |  |  | Side the master panes take on the current workspace: left, right, top, bottom or center. |
| `master_count` | int |  |  | How many panes are master panes on the current workspace, 1 to 9. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `tiling_mode` | string | "tiling" or "floating". |
| `layout_mode` | string | Which tiling layout is in effect: bsp, master-stack, scrolling, or "unknown" on a session no client has reported one for. |
| `master_ratio` | float | Fraction of the screen the master pane takes. |
| `master_position` | string | Side the master panes take on the current workspace, or "default" when the workspace uses the client's configured side. |
| `master_count` | int | How many panes are master panes on the current workspace, or 0 when the workspace uses the client's configured count. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-layout","params":{"session":"work","tiling":true,"equalize":true}}
```

```json
{"id":2,"verb":"set-layout","params":{"session":"work","master_position":"center","master_count":1}}
```

### `set-note`

Set the return note of a session, or of one pane with window: one short line that says what it is for or what comes next. Every client shows it, the dock shows it when a person comes back to the session, and it survives a reattach and a daemon restart.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name for a pane's note. Omit for the session's note. |
| `note` | string |  |  | The note. Newlines and tabs become spaces, other control characters are dropped, and it is cut to 200 bytes. Omit or pass an empty string to clear it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `type` | string | Always note_set. |
| `session` | string | The session's name. |
| `window` | string | The id of the pane the note is on, empty for the session's note. |
| `note` | string | The note as stored, empty when cleared. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-note","params":{"session":"work","note":"next: run the migration"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-note","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","note":""}}
```

### `set-option`

Set a configuration option. An attached client applies it live. The path and value are checked against the option registry, so a bad call fails instead of reporting success.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `key` | string |  |  | Option path, e.g. "appearance.sidebar.enabled". Call list-options for the full set. |
| `value` | string |  |  | New value, as a string. Booleans take true/false/on/off/1/0/yes/no. |
| `no_save` | bool |  |  | Apply the value without saving it. Later saves of other settings leave the key in the config files alone. The value stays until the client exits or the option is set again without no_save. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `key` | string | The option that was set. |
| `value` | string | The value recorded. |
| `applied` | bool | Whether an attached client applied it to the live display. |
| `no_save` | bool | The no_save the daemon used. A daemon that does not return it saves the value. |
| `reason` | string | Why applied is false, when it is. Present only then. |
| `deprecated` | string | Why this path is deprecated and what replaced it. Present only for a deprecated path. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-option","params":{"session":"work","key":"appearance.sidebar.enabled","value":"true"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-option","params":{"session":"work","key":"appearance.dockbar_position","value":"top"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-option","params":{"session":"work","key":"appearance.sidebar.enabled","value":"false","no_save":true}}
```

### `set-pane-grants`

Give a pane grants, or with reset the default of [agents.permissions]. Grants are read (its own session and fan group), write (type into its own session), fan (write in its fan group and start agents), respond (answer prompts without the person's nonce) and admin (everything else, as before grants existed). From outside every pane anything may be given. A pane may change only its own grants unless it holds admin, and may never give more than it holds, so a pane cannot widen itself. Refused over a link.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session of the pane. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | The pane, by window id or name. Omit from a pane to mean the caller's own. |
| `grants` | []string |  |  | The grants to give, or none for no grants at all. One of `read`, `write`, `fan`, `respond`, `files`, `task`, `admin` or `none`. |
| `reset` | bool |  | `false` | Give the pane the default of [agents.permissions] instead of grants of its own. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The pane's session. |
| `window` | string | The pane's window id. |
| `grants` | []string | What the pane holds now. |
| `explicit` | bool | False after reset: the pane holds the default and follows it when the config changes. |
| `previous` | []string | What the pane held before. |
| `previous_explicit` | bool | Whether what it held before was its own. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-pane-grants","params":{"session":"work","window":"a1b2c3d4","grants":["read","write"]}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-pane-grants","params":{"grants":["read"]}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-pane-grants","params":{"session":"work","window":"a1b2c3d4","reset":true}}
```

### `set-session-accent`

Set a session's accent colour. Every attached client shares it, and it survives a reattach.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `accent` | string |  |  | An ANSI colour name ("cyan", "bright blue") or a #rrggbb value, recorded verbatim. Omit or pass an empty string to clear it and let the client pick the session's colour. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-session-accent","params":{"session":"work","accent":"cyan"}}
```

### `set-session-name`

Set a session's display label. The session keeps its name for addressing, persistence and TUIOS_SESSION. To change the name, use rename-session.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `name` | string |  |  | Display label for the session. Omit or pass an empty string to clear it and fall back to the session name. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-session-name","params":{"session":"work","name":"Payments API"}}
```

### `set-session-order`

Put the sessions in an order. Every client lists them in it: the rail, go-to, tuios ls. It survives a daemon restart. A session the list leaves out, and one made later, goes after the named ones in creation order.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `order` | []string | yes |  | Session names in their new order. Every name must be a live session, or nothing changes. Repeats are dropped. |
| `skip_missing` | bool |  |  | Leave out a name that is no live session, rather than refusing the order. The result lists them as missing. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `type` | string | Always session_order_set. |
| `order` | []string | Every session's name, in the order the daemon lists them now. |
| `missing` | []string | With skip_missing, the names that were no live session. Omitted when there were none. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-session-order","params":{"order":["work"]}}
```

### `set-session-space`

File a session under a space, a named group of related sessions that tuios ls and the sidebar list together, or mark it global. A space exists while at least one session is in it. It survives a daemon restart with the session. A global session is in no space, and filing one is refused unless global is false.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `space` | string |  |  | The space's name. Surrounding whitespace is trimmed, and the rest follows the session name rules (no slash, backslash or control character) and is at most 64 bytes. Omit or pass an empty string to take the session out of any space. |
| `global` | bool |  |  | True marks the session global: it holds panes from more than one machine, is listed in its own group, and a new window in it asks which machine to run on. It takes the session out of its space, and is refused with a space. False takes the mark off before the space is set. Omit to leave the mark as it is. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `type` | string | Always session_space_set. |
| `session` | string | The session's name. |
| `space` | string | The space the session is in now, empty for none. |
| `global` | bool | Whether the session is global now. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-session-space","params":{"session":"work","space":"payments"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-session-space","params":{"session":"work","space":""}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-session-space","params":{"session":"work","global":true}}
```

### `set-task-dispatch`

Change task dispatch. mode sets a board to manual, pull or auto: in pull, an agent that finishes its card is given the board's next open card, as mail from tuios; in auto, the daemon starts the board's preset agent for each open card, in a new worktree, and mails it the card. preset sets that agent; auto needs one. cap bounds the agents at once. verify sets the command run in a card's worktree when its agent is done: a pass moves the card to review, a fail to todo. pause stops all dispatch at once, and any pane may pause. resume lifts the pause and every stop. quiet sets the hours when dispatch gives no card. A pane needs admin for every action but pause, and verify is refused from every pane. Refused over a link.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `project` | string |  |  | The project board, by origin URL. Omit for the session's own cards. |
| `action` | string | yes |  | What to change. One of `mode`, `pause`, `resume`, `quiet`, `preset`, `cap` or `verify`. |
| `mode` | string |  |  | With mode: the board's new mode. One of `manual`, `pull` or `auto`. |
| `quiet` | string |  |  | With quiet: a span of local time such as 22:00-07:00, or off. |
| `agent` | string |  |  | With preset or mode auto: the agent auto starts. A card's own agent comes first. |
| `model` | string |  |  | With preset: as start-agent takes it. |
| `effort` | string |  |  | With preset: as start-agent takes it. |
| `permission_mode` | string |  |  | With preset: as start-agent takes it. Omit for the agent's own default. |
| `machine` | string |  |  | With preset: here (the default), any, or a linked machine. |
| `cap` | int |  |  | With cap: agents at once in total, 0 for 3. |
| `machine_cap` | int |  |  | With cap: on one machine, 0 for 2. |
| `verify` | string |  |  | With verify: a shell line, or off. |
| `verify_timeout` | int |  |  | With verify: seconds, 0 for 600. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The attached client's nonce, to act as the person (by human). One that does not verify is refused with not_human. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `paused` | bool | True while dispatch is paused. |
| `paused_by` | string | Who paused it. |
| `stopped` | string | Why all dispatch stopped on its own, such as usage limit. Empty when it did not. |
| `quiet` | string | The quiet hours, local time, such as 22:00-07:00. |
| `quiet_now` | bool | True inside the quiet hours. |
| `boards` | []object | The boards with a mode or a stop: board, project or session, mode, failures (dispatched cards in a row that did not finish), stopped, verify and verify_timeout. |
| `cap` | int | Auto's agents at once, in total. |
| `machine_cap` | int | Auto's agents at once on one machine. |
| `running` | int | Auto's agents now. |
| `board` | object | The board the call named, also when it is manual. An auto board adds preset, running, waiting and note. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-task-dispatch","params":{"action":"mode","mode":"pull","project":"git@github.com:acme/api.git"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-task-dispatch","params":{"action":"pause"}}
```
