### `set-task-stage`

Move a task card to a stage by hand. The daemon also moves cards on its own ([tasks] auto_stage), only from the stages each fact moves a card from, so a card put in another stage by hand stays there. A move to done is a finish: the holder lets the card go, its block is lifted, and task-finished is sent. A move to the stage the card is in changes nothing. A pane changes only the card it holds unless it holds admin; the person, a shell, a link and a plugin change any card.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `id` | string | yes |  | The card, such as t1. |
| `stage` | string | yes |  | The stage to move the card to. One of `todo`, `plan`, `doing`, `verify`, `review`, `shipped` or `done`. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The attached client's nonce, to act as the person (by human). One that does not verify is refused with not_human. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | The card. |
| `session` | string | Its session. |
| `title` | string | Its title. |
| `preview` | string | The first line of its prompt, at most 80 characters. |
| `stage` | string | Its stage. One of `todo`, `plan`, `doing`, `verify`, `review`, `shipped` or `done`. |
| `stage_at` | int | When it entered the stage, Unix nanoseconds. |
| `holder` | object | The pane that holds it: window, name, harness, state (read from the pane now) and held_at. Null when no pane holds it. |
| `by` | string | Who added it: human, shell, the adding pane's window id, link:HOST or plugin:ID. |
| `at` | int | When it was added, Unix nanoseconds. |
| `blocked` | object | reason, by and at when the card is blocked, else null. |
| `fan` | object | fan and fan_id, for a card added in a fan's session. |
| `attachment_count` | int | How many attachments the card has. Absent for none. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-task-stage","params":{"session":"work","id":"t1","stage":"review"}}
```

### `set-window`

Change a window's name or minimized state. Pass only the fields to change.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window to change. Omit for the focused one. |
| `name` | string |  |  | New name. Pass an empty string to clear it and fall back to the shell's title. |
| `minimized` | bool |  |  | Minimize the window, or restore it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | Id of the window that changed. |
| `display_name` | string | The name shown now. After a clear this is the shell's title. |
| `minimized` | bool | Whether it is minimized now. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-window","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","name":"api tests","minimized":false}}
```

### `set-workspace-name`

Name a workspace. The workspace keeps its number for addressing. An unnamed workspace shows the name its active pane gave with OSC 1, else its number.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `workspace` | int | yes |  | Workspace number to name. |
| `name` | string |  |  | Label for the workspace. Omit or pass an empty string to clear it and fall back to the number. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-workspace-name","params":{"session":"work","workspace":2,"name":"review"}}
```

### `set-workspace-order`

Put the workspaces in an order, renumbering them: a workspace's number is its position. Each workspace takes its panes, name, focus and layout to its new number.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `order` | []int | yes |  | Workspace numbers in their new order. The workspaces named take the numbers they hold between them, smallest first, in this order: [3,1,2] makes 3 workspace 1, 1 workspace 2 and 2 workspace 3. A workspace the list omits keeps its number. Numbers outside the session's range and repeats are dropped. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"set-workspace-order","params":{"session":"work","order":[3,1,2]}}
```

### `ship-commit`

Stage every change in a pane's git work tree, untracked files included and ignored ones not, and commit it on the branch checked out there. git runs with the person's own identity, signing and hooks, and the message is used as given. Refused while the pane's agent is working or waiting on a prompt, unless force is given.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `message` | string |  |  | The commit message. Omit to use the pane's last prompt or turn summary. The call is refused when there is none. |
| `force` | bool |  | `false` | Commit while the agent is mid-turn. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The root of the work tree. |
| `branch` | string | The branch the commit is on. |
| `commit` | string | The new commit. |
| `message` | string | The message the commit has. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"ship-commit","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","message":"Add a retry to the client"}}
```

### `ship-merge`

Merge a worktree pane's branch into its base branch in the repository's main checkout. The main checkout must have that branch checked out and no uncommitted change. A merge that conflicts is aborted, the main checkout is left as it was, and the conflicting files are reported. Uncommitted changes in the worktree are not merged. Nothing is forced.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `into` | string |  |  | The branch to merge into. Omit for the base the worktree was made from, else the branch the main checkout is on. |
| `mode` | string |  | `merge` | merge fast-forwards when it can and makes a merge commit when it cannot. squash makes one commit. ff-only refuses a branch that cannot be fast-forwarded. One of `merge`, `squash` or `ff-only`. |
| `message` | string |  |  | The message of the merge or squash commit. Omit for git's own. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the worktree. |
| `worktree` | string | The root of the worktree. |
| `repo_root` | string | The main checkout merged in. |
| `branch` | string | The branch merged. |
| `into` | string | The branch merged into. |
| `mode` | string | merge, squash or ff-only. |
| `before` | string | The commit into was on before. |
| `after` | string | The commit into is on now. |
| `commits` | int | How many commits of the branch into did not have. |
| `fast_forward` | bool | Whether into only moved forward. |
| `up_to_date` | bool | Whether into already had every commit, so nothing changed. |
| `uncommitted` | int | How many uncommitted changes the worktree holds, which the merge did not take. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"ship-merge","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"ship-merge","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","into":"main","mode":"squash"}}
```

### `ship-pr`

Push a pane's branch and open a pull request for it with the gh CLI, as the person is logged in to gh. tuios holds no GitHub token. When the branch already has an open pull request, the push updates it and no new one is opened. The first call answers confirm_required with what would happen and a token. Call again with that token. A caller inside a pane, or on a restricted connection, also needs the person to allow it in the Inbox.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `remote` | string |  |  | The remote to push to. Omit for the one the branch follows, else the only one, else origin. |
| `confirm` | string |  |  | The token from the confirm_required answer to a call without it. The call goes ahead only with the token of what it would send now. |
| `request_id` | string |  |  | Come back for the person's answer to a question an earlier call put in the Inbox. |
| `wait` | int |  | `120000` | How long to wait for the person's answer in the Inbox, in milliseconds, at most 3600000. |
| `base` | string |  |  | The branch the pull request merges into. Omit for the base the worktree was made from, else the repository's default branch. |
| `title` | string |  |  | The title. With title and body both omitted, gh fills them from the commits. |
| `body` | string |  |  | The body. Needs title. |
| `draft` | bool |  | `false` | Open it as a draft. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The root of the work tree. |
| `branch` | string | The branch pushed. |
| `commit` | string | The commit pushed. |
| `remote` | string | The remote pushed to. |
| `created` | bool | Whether a new pull request was opened. False when one was already open. |
| `pr` | object or null | The pull request: number, url, state (open, merged or closed), checks (pass, fail or pending, omitted when there are none), passed, failed, pending, branch and checked_at (Unix nanoseconds). |
| `approved_by` | string | human when the person allowed it in the Inbox, omitted when the caller is the person. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"ship-pr","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","draft":true}}
```

### `ship-push`

Push a pane's branch to a remote and set it as the branch's upstream. Never forced. The first call answers confirm_required with what would be pushed and a token. Call again with that token to push. A caller inside a pane, or on a restricted connection, also needs the person to allow it in the Inbox, and the call waits for that answer.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `remote` | string |  |  | The remote to push to. Omit for the one the branch follows, else the only one, else origin. |
| `confirm` | string |  |  | The token from the confirm_required answer to a call without it. The call goes ahead only with the token of what it would send now. |
| `request_id` | string |  |  | Come back for the person's answer to a question an earlier call put in the Inbox. |
| `wait` | int |  | `120000` | How long to wait for the person's answer in the Inbox, in milliseconds, at most 3600000. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The root of the work tree. |
| `branch` | string | The branch pushed. |
| `commit` | string | The commit pushed. |
| `remote` | string | The remote pushed to. |
| `url` | string | Where the push went: the push URL's host and path, without user name, password or token. |
| `approved_by` | string | human when the person allowed it in the Inbox, omitted when the caller is the person. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"ship-push","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

### `ship-status`

Read the pull request of a pane's branch as tuios last saw it, and with refresh ask gh now. A worktree session with an open pull request is polled in the background while a client is attached. Off when gh is not installed.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `refresh` | bool |  | `false` | Ask gh now instead of reading what tuios last saw. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session of the pane. |
| `window` | string | The pane, by id. |
| `worktree` | string | The root of the work tree. |
| `branch` | string | The branch checked out there, empty on a detached HEAD. |
| `gh` | bool | Whether the gh CLI is installed. |
| `pr` | object or null | The pull request: number, url, state (open, merged or closed), checks (pass, fail or pending, omitted when there are none), passed, failed, pending, branch and checked_at (Unix nanoseconds). Null when tuios knows of none. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"ship-status","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

### `show-buffer`

Return the content of one paste buffer. A buffer can hold any bytes: data_b64 carries them all, and data carries them as text. From a pane this needs the read grant, and reaches only the buffers of the sessions the pane may read.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  |  | The buffer to show. Omit for the newest buffer tuios named, as tmux does: for the person, the newest of the person's own, and for a pane without admin, the newest of its own. |
| `version` | int |  |  | Act only while the buffer holds the content of this version, as list-buffers or show-buffer gave it. Otherwise the call answers no_buffer. |
| `encoding` | string |  |  | base64 to get data_b64 alone, without the text in data. One of `base64`. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | The buffer's name: bufferN for one tuios named, or the name set-buffer gave it. |
| `bytes` | int | How many bytes the buffer holds. |
| `created` | int | Unix-nano time the content was last set. |
| `version` | int | A number that changes each time the content is set. Pass it back as version to act only on this content. |
| `automatic` | bool | True when tuios named the buffer. |
| `sample` | string | The start of the content on one line, with control characters and bytes that are not UTF-8 shown as escapes. It never cuts a character. Print it as it is. |
| `pane` | string | The pane that owns the buffer, by its window name, or its short id once it is gone. Absent for the person's buffers. |
| `data` | string | The whole content as text. A byte that is not UTF-8 reads as U+FFFD here. |
| `data_b64` | string | The whole content, every byte, as base64. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"show-buffer"}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"show-buffer","params":{"name":"buffer3"}}
```

### `speed-test`

The far end of a link speed test. ping answers at once. sink takes bytes after the reply and drops them, then answers one line {bytes, ms}. source sends bytes of random data after the reply, then closes.

The connection stays open after the reply when `mode` is `sink` or `source`.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | string | yes |  | ping, sink or source. One of `ping`, `sink` or `source`. |
| `bytes` | int |  |  | How many bytes sink takes or source sends, at most 1 GiB. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `mode` | string | The mode. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"speed-test","params":{"mode":"ping"}}
```

### `split-window`

Split a pane and put a new one beside it. Works with no client attached, and turns tiling on when it is off. The new pane opens on the workspace of the pane it cuts, and starts in the caller's directory when cwd is left out.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window to split. Omit to split the caller's own pane, or outside a pane the focused one. |
| `direction` | string |  |  | Axis to cut on. The new pane goes right or below. Pass this or side. One of `horizontal` or `vertical`. |
| `side` | string |  |  | Side of the pane the new pane takes. Pass this or direction. One of `left`, `right`, `up` or `down`. |
| `ratio` | int |  |  | The new pane's share of the space, in percent, from 1 to 99. Omit for an even split. |
| `name` | string |  |  | Name for the new window. |
| `command` | []string |  |  | Argv to run in the new pane instead of a shell, as for new-window. |
| `cwd` | string |  |  | Directory to start in, as for new-window. |
| `focus` | bool |  | `true` | Focus the new pane. Pass false to leave the focus where it is. |
| `keep_open` | bool |  | `false` | Keep the pane after its process exits, as for new-window. |
| `close_on_exit` | bool |  | `false` | Close the pane when its process exits, as for new-window. |
| `env` | object |  |  | Environment variables for the pane's process, as for new-window. |
| `host` | string |  |  | Run the pane's process on another machine, as for new-window. |
| `shell_integration` | string |  |  | Whether the pane's shell starts with OSC 133 prompt marks on, as for new-window. One of `auto` or `off`. |
| `grants` | []string |  |  | What the new pane may do through tuios: read, write, fan, respond, admin, or none. Omit for the default of [agents.permissions], or, from a pane without admin, the calling pane's own grants. A pane may give only what it holds, and admin does not include respond. A window on another machine holds what that machine gives it. One of `read`, `write`, `fan`, `respond`, `files`, `task`, `admin` or `none`. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | Id of the pane the split created. |
| `direction` | string | The axis that was cut. |
| `side` | string | The side the new pane took. |
| `name` | string | The new pane's name. |
| `workspace` | int | Workspace the new pane is on. |
| `unplaced` | bool | True while a client has still to place the pane. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"split-window","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","direction":"vertical","name":"logs"}}
```

```json
{"id":2,"verb":"split-window","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","side":"down","ratio":30,"focus":false,"command":["tail","-f","app.log"]}}
```

| `target` | string | The client socket the link points at. Absent when there is no link. |


### `start-agent`

Start an agent in a new pane of a session and answer once it is ready for a prompt: its state reads idle or done, from a report, a hook, its screen or its title (unknown counts only for a harness that can never show idle). A pane on needs_input ends the wait at once with ready false and blocked_by, and is kept. With prompt, the first prompt is typed once it is ready, the way fan types it. A named session that does not exist is created. From another machine, address it with a host-qualified session and name the repository by repo_url.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session to open the pane in. Created when it does not exist. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session. Outside every pane, omit for the most recently active session, or a new one named after the agent when there is none. |
| `agent` | string |  |  | The agent, as one string: a harness id or program name (claude, codex, gemini), or any program, with its arguments after it. Split the way a shell splits words and exec'd directly; nothing is expanded. |
| `args` | []string |  |  | More arguments after the agent's own, as an argv. No shell parses them. |
| `name` | string |  |  | The window's name, which list-agents shows and -w takes, so the caller can address the agent as, say, reviewer. |
| `cwd` | string |  |  | The directory the agent starts in. Omit to use the calling pane's directory, or outside a pane the focused pane's. Pass cwd or a repository, not both. |
| `repo` | string |  |  | A directory inside a repository: the agent starts in its main checkout. |
| `workspace` | int |  |  | The workspace to open the pane on. Omit for the calling pane's workspace, else the one the session shows. |
| `focus` | bool |  | `false` | Focus the new pane. |
| `prompt` | string |  |  | A first prompt, typed once the agent is ready and checked the way fan checks it. |
| `ready_timeout` | int |  | `120000` | Milliseconds to wait for the agent to be ready. |
| `no_wait` | bool |  | `false` | Answer once the agent's process has started and has not exited within a second, without waiting for it to be ready (outcome started). after_ready and prompt are then sent in the background once it is ready, and their status reads pending. An agent that stays unready shows in the Inbox. |
| `env` | object |  |  | Environment variables for the agent, name to value, on top of the daemon's; PATH in it is where the program is looked up first. A program not on it is looked up on the daemon's PATH, the one a new pane gets, and then runs with that PATH (path_from). The rules are fan's. |
| `protocol` | string |  |  | Run the agent headless over a structured protocol instead of in its own TUI: acp (the Agent Client Protocol, for an agent command such as "opencode acp") or codex (the Codex app-server; app-server is added to the codex command). The pane runs tuios agent-proto, which shows the conversation as a transcript and reports the agent's state itself; the pane is ready on that report alone. Its permission requests are answered in the pane or, for a request one line shows whole, from the Inbox without [agents.approvals]. Omit for the agent's own TUI. One of `acp` or `codex`. |
| `task` | string |  |  | A task card of the session, such as t3, for the new pane to work on: it is claimed for the pane as soon as the pane exists, before the wait. The caller needs what claim-task needs, and the card must be held by no pane, not blocked and not done, checked before anything is started. The session must exist already. |
| `task_host` | string |  |  | With task: the linked machine that owns the card. The new pane claims it there. |
| `task_session` | string |  |  | The card's session: there with task_host, else another session of this daemon. The pane's close lets the card go. |
| `task_mail` | bool |  |  | With task: once the pane holds the card, mail it the card from tuios (title, prompt, and the commands that finish or release it), so nothing is typed into it. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | With task: the attached client's nonce, so the claim is the person's (by human). |
| `parent` | string |  |  | The agent pane the new agent reports to, by window id or a unique start of it. Omit it from an agent pane: that pane is the parent. A pane may name itself or a pane it could type into, and the person may name any agent pane. A chain of agents stops at 8: a deeper start is refused. |
| `model` | string |  |  | The model, passed with the flag the harness's manifest names ([launch] model_flag). One word, not starting with -. Refused for a harness with no model flag. |
| `effort` | string |  |  | The effort, one of the harness's [launch] efforts, passed with its effort flag. Refused for a harness with no effort flag. |
| `permission_mode` | string |  |  | The permission mode, one of the harness's [launch] permissions, passed with its permission flag. From an agent pane it is held to the caller's own mode. Refused for a harness with no permission flag. |
| `preset` | string |  |  | An [agents.presets] entry of this daemon's config to start from. A param the call passes wins over the preset's; args, add_dirs and after_ready are the preset's then the call's; env is the preset's with the call's on top; grants the call passes replace the preset's. An unknown name is refused with the names there are. |
| `add_dirs` | []string |  |  | More directories the agent may work in, each passed with the harness's [launch] add_dir_flag (Claude Code: --add-dir), before args. ~ is this machine's home folder. Refused for a harness with no such flag. |
| `after_ready` | []string |  |  | Steps sent once the agent is ready, in order and before prompt: a step that is one key name (Enter, Escape, Tab, C-c) is that key, anything else is typed as text. A step is refused while the pane waits on a prompt of its own, which stops the rest. |
| `grants` | []string |  |  | What the new pane may do through tuios: read, write, fan, respond, admin, or none. Omit for the default of [agents.permissions], or, from a pane without admin, the calling pane's own grants. A pane may give only what it holds, and admin does not include respond. A window on another machine holds what that machine gives it. One of `read`, `write`, `fan`, `respond`, `files`, `task`, `admin` or `none`. |
| `repo_url` | string |  |  | The repository by its origin URL, for a caller on another machine. This daemon finds its own checkout whose origin is the same repository. Pass repo or repo_url, not both. |
| `repos_root` | string |  |  | With repo_url, the directory on this machine to look under, and to clone into. Absolute, or starting with ~/. Omit to look under ~/src, ~/dev, ~/code, ~/projects, ~/repos, ~/git, ~/work, ~/go/src, the home directory itself and tuios's own clone directory. |
| `clone` | bool |  | `false` | With repo_url, clone the repository when no checkout is found. Only https, ssh and git URLs are cloned. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | The session the pane is in. |
| `session_id` | string | Id of the session. |
| `created_session` | bool | True when the session was created by this call. |
| `pty_id` | string | Id of the new pane's PTY. |
| `workspace` | int | The workspace the pane is on. |
| `cwd` | string | The directory the agent started in, empty for the default. |
| `cloned` | bool | True when the repository was cloned by this call. Absent otherwise. |
| `parent` | object | The parent agent pane, session and window, when the new agent has one. Absent otherwise. |
| `root` | object | The first agent of the parent chain, session and window, when the new agent has a parent. Absent otherwise. |
| `window_id` | string | The new pane. |
| `name` | string | Its name. |
| `agent` | string | The harness id, from the manifest or detection, empty when nothing recognises the program. |
| `command` | string | The command that was started. With protocol, the agent's command, which tuios agent-proto runs. |
| `protocol` | string | The protocol, when one was asked for. Absent otherwise. |
| `ready` | bool | True when the agent showed it is at its prompt. |
| `ready_by` | string | The evidence: idle or done, or quiet for unknown on a harness that cannot show idle. |
| `state` | string | The pane's agent state when the wait ended. |
| `outcome` | string | How the wait ended. started is no_wait's answer: the process runs and readiness was not waited for. exited is no_wait's answer when the program exited within a second. One of `ready`, `blocked`, `timeout`, `window_closed`, `session_closed`, `shutdown`, `started` or `exited`. |
| `path_from` | string | Which PATH found the program: caller, the PATH in env, or daemon, the daemon's PATH, which the agent then runs with. One of `caller` or `daemon`. |
| `blocked_by` | string | approval or question, when the outcome is blocked. |
| `reason` | string | Why it is not ready, when it is not. |
| `prompt_status` | string | With prompt: sent, stalled or not_sent, or pending with no_wait. |
| `prompt_note` | string | Why a prompt was not sent or looks stalled. |
| `after_ready_status` | string | With after_ready: sent, stopped or not_sent, or pending with no_wait. |
| `after_ready_note` | string | Why the after_ready steps were not all sent. |
| `task` | object | With task: the card as claimed for the pane, as list-tasks gives a row. |
| `task_note` | string | With task: why the card was not claimed after all, such as another pane taking it while the pane opened. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"start-agent","params":{"session":"work","agent":"claude","name":"reviewer","cwd":"/src/api"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"start-agent","params":{"session":"work","agent":"codex --model o5","name":"tests","cwd":"/src/api","prompt":"Run the test suite and fix what fails.","ready_timeout":300000}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"start-agent","params":{"session":"api","agent":"codex","repo_url":"git@github.com:acme/api.git","repos_root":"~/src","args":["--model","o4"],"prompt":"Fix the flaky test."}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"start-agent","params":{"session":"work","agent":"opencode acp","protocol":"acp","name":"helper","cwd":"/src/api","prompt":"Summarise the open TODOs."}}
```

### `stash-get`

Read one stashed file back as bytes, so it can cross a link. Only a path this session's stash printed is served, and a file over 8 MB is refused.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `path` | string |  |  | A stored path, as stash-put or stash-list printed it. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The stored path. |
| `name` | string | The stored file's base name. |
| `hash` | string | The sha256 of the content, hex. |
| `bytes` | int | How many bytes the content holds. |
| `media_type` | string | The type read from the extension. |
| `kind` | string | image or file. One of `image` or `file`. |
| `content` | string | The bytes, base64. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"stash-get","params":{"session":"work","path":"/run/user/1000/tuios/stash/<id>/<hash>.png"}}
```

### `stash-list`

List the files in a session's store, with the size of each, whether a message still names it, and how many the store has evicted. The oldest file no message names is the next one dropped when the session hits its cap.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `dir` | string | The directory holding this session's stored files. |
| `entries` | []object | One entry per file, oldest use first: path, name, hash, bytes, media_type, kind, source, stored_at, referenced, missing. |
| `total` | int | How many files are stored. |
| `bytes` | int | How many bytes they take together. |
| `evicted` | int | How many files this store has deleted to make room since the session started. |
| `max_file_bytes` | int | The per-file cap in bytes. |
| `max_bytes` | int | The per-session cap in bytes. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"stash-list","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

### `stash-put`

Copy a file into the session's own file store and answer with the stored path. The stored file lives as long as the session and is deleted when the session is killed or the daemon stops. Attach the stored path to a message like any other path.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `path` | string |  |  | Absolute path to an existing regular file on the daemon's host. The daemon opens and copies it as the user that started it. With content, the daemon does not open it. It only names the stored file. |
| `content` | string |  |  | The file's bytes, base64, for a file on another machine. At most 8 MB decoded. The path then gives the extension and the source label. From a linked machine, content is required. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The stored path. Attach this, or hand it to another agent. |
| `hash` | string | The sha256 of the content, hex. The stored name is this plus the source's extension. |
| `bytes` | int | How many bytes were stored. |
| `media_type` | string | The type read from the extension, the same way an attachment's is. |
| `kind` | string | image or file, from the media type. One of `image` or `file`. |
| `stored_at` | int | Unix-nano time the file was stored, or last asked for. |
| `deduped` | bool | True when these bytes were already stored. Nothing new was written and the path is the one that already existed. |
| `evicted` | int | How many files this put deleted to make room. |
| `evictions` | int | How many files this session's store has deleted in total. A number that moved means a file stashed earlier may be gone. |
| `session_bytes` | int | How many bytes this session's store holds now. |
| `session_entries` | int | How many files this session's store holds now. |
| `max_file_bytes` | int | The per-file cap in bytes. |
| `max_bytes` | int | The per-session cap in bytes. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"stash-put","params":{"session":"work","path":"/tmp/flame.png"}}
```

### `stop-recording`

Stop a pane's recording. Answers once the file is closed.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | The file written. |
| `window` | string | The id of the pane recorded. |
| `events` | int | How many events the file holds, the snapshot included. |
| `bytes` | int | The size of the file. |
| `seconds` | float | The time of the last event. |
| `reason` | string | Why the recording ended: stopped here, or what ended it first. |
| `started_at` | string | When the recording started, RFC 3339 in UTC. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"stop-recording","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

### `stream-pane`

Stream one pane as bytes, for a client that is not tuios. The reply is one JSON line, and after it the connection carries binary frames both ways: a type byte, a 4-byte big-endian length, then the payload. From the daemon: S snapshot (u64 seq, u16 cols, u16 rows, then bytes that paint the pane on a fresh emulator of that size), O output (u64 seq after the frame's last byte, then the pane's bytes), R resize (u64 seq, u16 cols, u16 rows), E error (JSON code and message, for a refused frame or a lease that was not renewed; the stream goes on), X exit (the reason: exited N when the pane's program exited with status N, killed N when signal N ended it, closed when its window closed first, or refused: … when the caller may no longer read it; the connection then closes). From the client: I input (at most 64 KiB of bytes typed into the pane, checked as send-text is), L lease (u16 cols, u16 rows, 0 0 to release; send it again every 10 seconds, or the lease ends after 30). Skip a frame type you do not know: read its length and drop that many bytes. At most 4 I frames wait for a pane that does not read, and the next gets E busy. The stream never changes the pane's size or the session's. A lease holds the pane at most at its size while the stream is open, and the pane goes back to the size its clients asked for when the lease ends.

The connection stays open after the reply.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | The pane to stream: a window id or name. |
| `from_seq` | int |  |  | The seq the client reached on an earlier stream of this pane. With boot_id, the stream resumes there when the daemon still holds the bytes after it. |
| `boot_id` | string |  |  | The boot_id of the stream from_seq came from. A different one means the daemon restarted, and the stream starts with a snapshot. |
| `lease_cols` | int |  |  | Hold the pane at most this many columns wide while the stream is open. Needs lease_rows. It needs what resize needs. |
| `lease_rows` | int |  |  | Hold the pane at most this many rows high while the stream is open. Needs lease_cols. |
| `session_id` | string |  |  | The session by its id, which a rename does not change. It wins over session. The reply carries it. |
| `fit` | bool |  |  | Make the lease a viewer's: while no TUI client shows the session, the pane takes the size of the largest fit lease held, and goes back to its own size when the last one ends. While a client shows the session, a fit lease only holds the pane smaller. |
| `viewer` | object |  |  | The terminal the stream shows the pane in: bg, fg (#rrggbb), palette (sixteen comma-separated #rrggbb), kitty and sixel (bool). While no TUI client shows the session, the daemon answers the pane's OSC 10, 11 and 4, DA1 and kitty graphics queries from the newest viewer's report. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `mode` | string | resume: the first frames are output from from_seq. snapshot: the first frame is S. One of `resume` or `snapshot`. |
| `seq` | int | The stream position the first frame starts at. |
| `cols` | int | The pane's width at seq. |
| `rows` | int | The pane's height at seq. |
| `boot_id` | string | This daemon start. Send it back with from_seq to resume. |
| `session` | string | The pane's session. |
| `session_id` | string | The pane's session id, which a rename does not change. |
| `window` | string | The pane's window id. |
| `title` | string | The pane's title. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"stream-pane","params":{"session":"work","window":"build"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"stream-pane","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","from_seq":48211,"boot_id":"<from the last reply>","lease_cols":45,"lease_rows":20}}
```

### `subscribe`

Open a long-lived event stream on this connection. Without after_seq, events start at the moment of subscription. With after_seq, the stream first replays the retained events after that seq, so a reconnecting subscriber gets what it missed, and sends a gap marker with a reason when it cannot.

The connection stays open after the reply.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Only deliver events from this session. Omit for events from every session. Unlike most verbs, an omitted session does not mean the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Only deliver events about this window id. Omit for every window. |
| `types` | []string |  |  | Only deliver these event types. Omit for all of them. One of `window-created`, `window-closed`, `window-exit`, `window-retitled`, `window-focused`, `window-moved`, `window-minimized`, `window-restored`, `workspace-switched`, `workspace-renamed`, `layout-changed`, `agent-state`, `agent-seen`, `agent-message`, `output`, `bell`, `notification`, `mode-changed`, `session-created`, `session-closed`, `session-labelled`, `client-session-changed`, `gap`, `attention`, `host-changed`, `prompt`, `command-started`, `command-finished`, `agent-activity`, `transcript`, `transfer`, `transfer-progress`, `transfer-file`, `daemon-upgraded`, `tml-event`, `tml-surface`, `task-claimed`, `task-finished`, `task-changed`, `task-dispatch`, `keep-awake`, `worktree-removed` or `theme`. |
| `queue` | int |  | `256` | Buffered events before the stream marks a gap. A size above 4096 is lowered to 4096. |
| `after_seq` | int |  |  | Resume: replay the retained events with a seq above this one before streaming live. Pass the seq of the last event received. 0 replays everything the daemon still holds. Output events are not retained. |
| `boot_id` | string |  |  | The boot_id the after_seq came with. If the daemon has restarted since, the stream starts with a gap marker with reason boot_changed instead of a replay. Needs after_seq. |
| `hosts` | bool |  | `false` | Also deliver the agent-state, session-created and session-closed events this daemon relays from its linked hosts, each carrying host, and let session and window match events of other machines. Without it an event about another machine reaches only a subscriber that names no session or window, and only an attention or host-changed event. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `seq` | int | The last seq assigned when the stream went live. Every live event has a higher seq, and every replayed event has this seq or lower. |
| `boot_id` | string | Random id of this daemon start. Keep it with the seq, and pass both back to resume. |
| `replayed` | int | With after_seq only: how many events are replayed before the live stream. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"subscribe","params":{"session":"work","types":["window-created","window-closed"]}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"subscribe","params":{"types":["agent-state"],"after_seq":118,"boot_id":"9f2c41d07a3e8b65"}}
```

Swap two tiled panes of one workspace. Works with no client attached. The focus stays where it is.

| `window` | string |  |  | One pane. Omit for the caller's own pane, or outside a pane the focused one. |
| `with` | string | yes |  | The other pane. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | Id of the first pane. |
| `with` | string | Id of the other pane. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"swap-window","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","with":"review"}}
```

### `switch-session`

Switch an attached client to another session in place, as go-to does. The client is the one named by client, else the one showing session, else the one showing the caller's own session from a pane, else the only one attached.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  |  | Session to switch to. |
| `host` | string |  |  | Machine from the client's [hosts] table that holds the session. Omit, or local, for this daemon. |
| `create` | bool |  | `false` | Create the session when it does not exist. |
| `cwd` | string |  |  | With create, the directory the new session's windows start in. |
| `session` | string |  |  | Session the client to switch shows. |
| `client` | string |  |  | Id of the client to switch, from list-clients. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `session` | string | Session the client now shows. |
| `host` | string | Machine the session is on. Absent for this daemon. |
| `client_id` | string | Id of the client that switched. |
| `created` | bool | True when create made the session on this daemon. |
| `already` | bool | True when the client already showed the session. Nothing changed. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"switch-session","params":{"name":"api"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"switch-session","params":{"name":"api","create":true,"cwd":"/src/api"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"switch-session","params":{"name":"api","host":"build","session":"work"}}
```

| `state` | object | The state now. |
| `version` | int | The version: it counts every change of the tree or the state. |
| `window_id` | string | The window the surface is drawn in. |

### `tml-input`

Report what the person did on a TML surface: the state its local actions changed, and a close. The attached client sends it, with its attach nonce.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `surface` | string |  |  | The surface id. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The attach nonce of the person's client. |
| `ops` | []object |  |  | A JSON Patch (RFC 6902) on the surface's state. |
| `close` | bool |  | `false` | Close the surface. |
| `value` | any |  |  | With close: the value. Omit when the person dismissed the surface. |
| `events` | []object |  |  | The emit actions the person ran, each {name, value}. Each becomes a tml-event for the owner. |
| `actions` | []object |  |  | The pane() actions the person ran, each {verb, params}: focus, new-window or send-text. Each runs with the grants of the pane that opened the surface, never the person's. |
| `view` | object |  |  | The client's view state, as loom keeps it: open tabs, cursor rows, scroll places, sorts, filters and folds, without typed text. capture-pane draws the surface with the last view reported. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `version` | int | The surface's version after the change. |
| `actions` | []object | Each pane() action's result: verb, ok, and error and code when it was refused. |

```json

```

### `tml-list`

List the open TML surfaces of a session.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `surfaces` | []object | Each surface: surface, window_id, title, owner and version. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-list","params":{"session":"work"}}
```

### `tml-open`

Show a TML document as a popup or a pane. Every attached client draws it, and the daemon keeps it while nobody is attached.

The connection stays open after the reply when `events` is `true`.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `doc` | string |  |  | The document: TML text or TML JSON as a string, or a TML JSON object. It is checked as tml-validate checks it, and a document with errors is refused with invalid_tml and the problem list. |
| `target` | string |  | `popup` | Where to show it: popup, a floating box over the panes that needs an attached client, or pane, a tiled pane. One of `popup` or `pane`. |
| `name` | string |  |  | Name for the window. Omit to use the document's title. |
| `width` | string |  | `80%` | Popup width, in cells ("60") or as a share of the pane region ("60%"). Popup only. |
| `height` | string |  | `60%` | Popup height, in cells ("20") or as a share of the pane region ("50%"). Popup only. |
| `workspace` | int |  |  | Workspace to open it on. Omit for the current one. |
| `wait` | bool |  | `false` | Keep the call open until the surface closes, and return the close value in a tml_result. |
| `timeout` | int |  | `0` | With wait: milliseconds to wait before failing with the timeout code. The surface stays open. 0 waits as long as it is open. |
| `events` | bool |  | `false` | Keep the connection and stream the surface's tml-event events on it after the answer, as subscribe does: each emit, and the close as the last one. Not with wait. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `surface` | string | Id of the surface. Use it to patch, replace, read or close the surface. |
| `seq` | int | With events: the last event number when the stream went live, as subscribe answers. |
| `boot_id` | string | With events: the daemon start that numbers the events. |
| `window_id` | string | Id of the window the surface is drawn in. |
| `target` | string | popup or pane. |
| `workspace` | int | Workspace the surface was opened on. |
| `value` | any | With wait: the value the surface closed with. null when the person dismissed it. |
| `reason` | string | With wait: closed when the person ran a close action, so the value is the person's answer; program when a tml-close gave the value; dismissed when the window closed without one. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-open","params":{"session":"work","doc":"<tml version=\"1\" title=\"Hi\"><text>Hello</text></tml>"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-open","params":{"doc":{"type":"tml","version":1,"title":"Build","children":[{"type":"progress","value":0.4}]},"target":"pane"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-open","params":{"doc":"<tml version=\"1\"><button key=\"y\" on-press=\"close('yes')\">Yes</button></tml>","wait":true}}
```

### `tml-patch`

Change a TML surface's state with a JSON Patch (RFC 6902). Only the parts of the surface that read the changed paths are drawn again.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `surface` | string |  |  | The surface id tml-open answered with. |
| `ops` | []object | yes |  | The JSON Patch: add, remove, replace, move, copy and test operations on the state. A test that does not match refuses the whole patch, so read the state again with tml-get. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `version` | int | The surface's version after the patch. |
| `changed` | []string | The JSON Pointers the patch changed. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-patch","params":{"surface":"tml-4f1a9c02","ops":[{"op":"replace","path":"/failed","value":0}]}}
```

### `tml-place`

Move a TML surface between a popup and a tiled pane, as the person chose. The surface keeps its state, so a form keeps what the person typed. Needs the attach nonce of a client.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `surface` | string |  |  | The surface id. |
| `target` | string |  |  | popup or pane. One of `popup` or `pane`. |
| `human_nonce` | string |  |  | The attach nonce of the client the person uses. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | The surface's window. |
| `target` | string | Where the surface is now. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-place","params":{"surface":"tml-4f1a9c02","target":"pane","human_nonce":"..."}}
```

### `tml-replace`

Swap a TML surface's tree. Each client keeps its view state (the open tab, the cursor row) for the nodes whose ids stay.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `surface` | string |  |  | The surface id. |
| `doc` | string |  |  | The new document, as tml-open takes it. |
| `keep_state` | bool |  | `false` | Keep the state the surface has now instead of the new document's state. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `version` | int | The surface's version after the swap. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-replace","params":{"surface":"tml-4f1a9c02","doc":"<tml version=\"1\"><text>Done</text></tml>"}}
```

### `tml-validate`

Check a TML document as tml-open would. Needs no session.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `doc` | string |  |  | The document: TML text or TML JSON as a string, or a TML JSON object. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `warnings` | []object | The warnings, each with at, code and message. A document with errors fails with invalid_tml instead. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"tml-validate","params":{"doc":"<tml version=\"1\"><text>Hello</text></tml>"}}
```

### `transfer-answer`

Answer a copy in state conflict: what to do with files that are there and differ. Answer one file, several, or every file with all. The copy goes on when every file it waits on has an answer.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes |  | The transfer's id. |
| `choice` | string | yes |  | replace the file there, keep both (the copy gets a new name), or skip the file. One of `replace`, `keep-both` or `skip`. |
| `rel` | string |  |  | The file, as the row's conflicts name it. |
| `rels` | []string |  |  | Several files. |
| `all` | bool |  |  | This answer for every file of the copy, now and later. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | The transfer's id. |
| `state` | string | queued, running, verifying, waiting (for a machine that went away), paused, conflict (waits for transfer-answer), done, failed or cancelled. One of `queued`, `running`, `verifying`, `waiting`, `paused`, `conflict`, `done`, `failed` or `cancelled`. |
| `size` | int | Bytes to copy in all. |
| `done` | int | Bytes copied, or found to be there already. |
| `wire` | int | Bytes that crossed a link, fewer than done when they were compressed. |
| `rate` | float | Bytes per second over the last five seconds. |
| `eta_ms` | int | Time left, once two seconds of data came. |
| `resumed_from` | int | Where the copy went on from after a machine came back. Absent when it never had to. |
| `verified` | bool | The copy's sha256 matched the original's. |
| `final` | string | Where the file is now, which keep-both can change. |
| `same` | int | Files that were there with the same bytes, which were not copied again. |
| `conflicts_skipped` | int | Files that were there and differed, left as they were. |
| `conflicts` | []object | In state conflict: the files that wait for an answer, rel, path, src_size, src_mtime, dst_size, dst_mtime. |
| `error` | string | What happened and what to do, for failed and waiting. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"transfer-answer","params":{"id":"3f9a1c2b7d00","choice":"keep-both","all":true}}
```

| `active` | int | Transfers that are not finished. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"transfer-list"}
```

### `transfer-start`

Copy a file or a folder between this machine and a host, or between two hosts. The daemon runs the copy, so a client may quit, and a daemon restart goes on with it. A machine that goes away pauses it, and it goes on from where it stopped when the machine is back. Every file's sha256 is checked before it is put in place, and it keeps the original's permission bits and modification time. A file that arrives over a link gets the receiving machine's umask and never group or world write. A file that is there with the same sha256 is not copied again. A destination that exists answers file_exists unless conflict says what to do. A destination another copy writes now answers busy. Each change is a transfer event on the event stream.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `src` | object | yes |  | What to copy: {host, path}. host is a name from [hosts], or empty for this machine. path is absolute or starts with ~. |
| `dst` | object | yes |  | Where it goes: the full path of the copy, or with place a folder: {host, path}. host is a name from [hosts], or empty for this machine. path is absolute or starts with ~. |
| `move` | bool |  |  | Remove the original once the copy is checked. |
| `conflict` | string |  | `fail` | When dst exists: replace it, keep both (the copy gets a new name), merge a folder into the folder there, skip a file that differs, ask (the copy waits in state conflict for transfer-answer), or fail. One of `replace`, `keep-both`, `merge`, `skip`, `ask` or `fail`. |
| `each` | string |  |  | In a folder copy into a folder that is there: what to do with each file that is there and differs. Omit to follow conflict, which for merge and replace is replace. One of `replace`, `keep-both`, `skip` or `ask`. |
| `place` | string |  |  | auto reads dst as cp does: a folder that is there gets the copy inside it, and anything else is the new name. into needs dst to be a folder that is there. A dst that ends in / with auto is into. contents copies what a folder holds into dst, and a file as auto does. One of `auto`, `into` or `contents`. |
| `perms` | bool |  | `true` | Give each file the original's permission bits. |
| `times` | bool |  | `true` | Give each file the original's modification time. |
| `compress` | bool |  | `true` | Compress the bytes on a link when they compress well. |
| `rate_limit` | int |  | `0` | At most this many bytes a second. 0 for no limit. |
| `keep_from` | bool |  | `true` | keep-both names a copy from another machine "name (from HOST).ext". false numbers it, "name 2.ext". |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | The transfer's id. |
| `state` | string | queued, running, verifying, waiting (for a machine that went away), paused, conflict (waits for transfer-answer), done, failed or cancelled. One of `queued`, `running`, `verifying`, `waiting`, `paused`, `conflict`, `done`, `failed` or `cancelled`. |
| `size` | int | Bytes to copy in all. |
| `done` | int | Bytes copied, or found to be there already. |
| `wire` | int | Bytes that crossed a link, fewer than done when they were compressed. |
| `rate` | float | Bytes per second over the last five seconds. |
| `eta_ms` | int | Time left, once two seconds of data came. |
| `resumed_from` | int | Where the copy went on from after a machine came back. Absent when it never had to. |
| `verified` | bool | The copy's sha256 matched the original's. |
| `final` | string | Where the file is now, which keep-both can change. |
| `same` | int | Files that were there with the same bytes, which were not copied again. |
| `conflicts_skipped` | int | Files that were there and differed, left as they were. |
| `conflicts` | []object | In state conflict: the files that wait for an answer, rel, path, src_size, src_mtime, dst_size, dst_mtime. |
| `error` | string | What happened and what to do, for failed and waiting. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"transfer-start","params":{"src":{"host":"build","path":"~/out/image.iso"},"dst":{"path":"~/Downloads/image.iso"}}}
```

### `unsubscribe`

Close this connection's event stream.

No params.

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"unsubscribe"}
```

### `verify-fan`

Run one check in every attempt of a fan: a window named verify in each sibling session runs the command with sh -c and records whether it passed. The window holds no grants, so the check cannot call tuios. It closes when the check passes and stays open, so the output can be read, when it fails. The command is always the caller's; none is read from the repository. A check still running in a sibling is stopped first.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Any session of the fan. Omit for the most recently active session. |
| `command` | string | yes |  | The command to run, as a shell line, at most 4096 bytes. |
| `timeout_ms` | int |  |  | How long a check may run before it counts as failed and its window is closed, in milliseconds. Omit for no limit. |
| `env` | object |  |  | Environment variables for the check, name to value, on top of the daemon's, with the rules of fan's env. The tuios CLI sends its PATH. A call from another machine may not pass env. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `group` | string | The fan's group. |
| `command` | string | The command as it runs. |
| `sessions` | []string | The sessions a check was started in. Results reach clients as the verify field of each session's worktree, and compare-fan reports them. |
| `skipped` | []object | Siblings no check was started in: session and reason. Omitted when none. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"verify-fan","params":{"session":"api-fan-retry-1","command":"go test ./..."}}
```

### `wait-dir`

Wait until the names in a directory on this machine change: a file added, removed or renamed. The daemon that owns a window on another machine asks it, so the rail's file section can follow that machine's disk. It answers changed false when the timeout ends first.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `dir` | string |  |  | The directory to watch. |
| `timeout` | int |  |  | Milliseconds to wait. Omit for 30 seconds. |
| `after` | string |  |  | The names value of the last answer. When the names differ from it, the wait answers at once with changed true. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `dir` | string | The directory watched. |
| `changed` | bool | The names changed before the timeout ended. |
| `names` | string | A short sum of the names in the directory, empty when they cannot be read or there are more than 100000. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-dir","params":{"dir":"/tmp","timeout":1}}
```

### `wait-for`

Block until a condition matches, or fail with the timeout code.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `condition` | string |  |  | Condition to wait for. One of `session-exists`, `window-output`, `window-exit`, `window-idle`, `agent-state`, `agent-message` or `command-finished`. |
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. session-exists requires it: it is the session to wait for. For the other conditions, omit it from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window id or name. Omit to target the focused window. |
| `any_session` | bool |  | `false` | For agent-state only: watch every session on the daemon, including ones created during the wait. Takes no session or window. The result names the session that matched. |
| `pattern` | string |  |  | Regular expression, required by window-output. |
| `source` | string |  | `recent` | Which buffer window-output matches against. The default includes scrollback, so output that has already scrolled past still matches. One of `visible` or `recent`. |
| `new` | bool |  | `false` | For window-output: match only the output the pane writes after the wait starts. Text already on the screen or in the scrollback does not match. A program that redraws its screen writes its text again, and that text matches. Takes no source. |
| `idle` | int |  | `500` | Milliseconds of silence that count as idle, for window-idle. |
| `until` | string |  |  | Agent state(s) to wait for, comma-separated, required by agent-state. With no window, any window in the session reaching one of them matches. One of `none`, `working`, `needs_input`, `idle`, `done`, `errored`, `unknown` or `limit_reached`. |
| `thread` | int |  |  | Narrow agent-message to one thread. Pass any message id in the thread. A thread the ring holds nothing from never matches. |
| `command_seq` | int |  |  | For command-finished with a window: match once the pane has finished more commands than this, which is already true when the command finished before the wait. Read it from list-windows or a run timeout. Without it, the next command to finish after the wait starts matches. |
| `timeout` | int |  | `30000` | Milliseconds to wait before failing with the timeout code, at most 86400000 (24 hours). The wait also ends when the caller closes the connection. |
| `select` | string |  |  | A selector: space-separated key:value terms, all of which must match, each with comma-separated alternatives. Keys: harness (id or program name), state, needs:you, session (glob), group (fan-out group, glob), host (local or a host name, glob), name (window name, glob), cwd (the directory or under it; ~ is home), task (a task card the pane holds, by id, glob), stage (the stage of a card the pane holds), parent and root (the parent agent pane, or the first of its chain, by window id or a start of it). For agent-state only: watch the agent panes it matches, in every session, including panes that open during the wait. Takes no session, window or any_session. Put the state to wait for in until, not in the selector. |
| `every` | bool |  | `false` | With select: match only when at least one pane matches and every matched pane is in one of the until states, and answer with all of them in panes. Without it, the first matched pane to reach one matches. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `condition` | string | The condition that matched. |
| `window` | string | The window that matched, for a condition about a window. |
| `exit_code` | int | For window-exit and command-finished: the exit status, -1 when a signal ended the process. Omitted when it is not known, such as for a window on another machine. |
| `signal` | int | For window-exit: the signal that ended the process. Omitted when it exited on its own. |
| `new` | bool | For window-output: true when the wait matched only output written after it started. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"window-output","session":"work","pattern":"done","timeout":10000}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"agent-state","session":"work","until":"needs_input,idle"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"agent-message","session":"work","window":"$TUIOS_PANE_ID"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"agent-message","session":"work","window":"$TUIOS_PANE_ID","thread":12}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"agent-state","any_session":true,"until":"needs_input"}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"command-finished","session":"work","window":"build","command_seq":4,"timeout":600000}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"agent-state","select":"group:fan/add-retry","until":"idle,done","every":true}}
```

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"wait-for","params":{"condition":"window-output","session":"work","window":"build","pattern":"BUILD OK","new":true}}
```

### `zoom-window`

Zoom a pane to fill its workspace, or end the zoom. Works with no client attached. Zooming a pane on the workspace the session shows focuses it.

| Param | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | string |  |  | Session name. Omit from a pane for the pane's own session, and from outside every pane for the most recently active one. |
| `window` | string |  |  | Window to zoom. Omit for the caller's own pane, or outside a pane the focused one. |
| `zoom` | bool |  |  | True to zoom, false to end the zoom on the window's workspace. Omit to toggle. |

| Result field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `window_id` | string | Id of the window. |
| `zoomed` | bool | Whether the window is zoomed now. |
| `changed` | bool | False when the zoom was already as asked. |

```json
{"id":1,"verb":"zoom-window","params":{"session":"work","window":"build","zoom":true}}
```
